rūpāvati (nāṭakam) vi.kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār

rUpAvati (play) by V.K. cUryanArayaNa cAstiriyar In tamil script, unicode/utf-8 format

------------------------------------------------------------------------

tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ varalāṟu (vi.kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāstiriyār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūṟṟokuti - iraṇṭām pakuti ) 

"rūpāvati" allatu "kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa makaḷ"

Dedication :

--------
urimiyuri
eḻucīrkkaḻi neṭilaṭi yāciriya viruttam.

----------------

INTRODUCTION

Of the time- honoured trifurcation of Tamil Literature into Verse,
Music, and the Drama, the last has few or no representatives while the
first and the second have many. The positive want of ancient dramatic
literature has been accounted for by the lovers of Tamil in various
ways, which seem more or less fabulous to those who have any tincture of
English education. That the Tamil speaking people have included the
histrionic art among the degraded arts of life, fit to be pursued by the
wandering nomads, or by the dregs of societies, seems to point to the
conclusion that the art in due course of time have degenerated and
fallen into disrepute or lacked cultivation at the hands of men of
genius. In either case, any attempt I that direction in these days of
western culture can not but add to the reputation of one who, with
necessary intellectual equipment, energy, and zeal, devotes his nights
and days to it.
In Madras, as in the Mofussil, we often come across travelling troops of
actors amusing audience with variety of plays based on the three great
magazines* of Poetic fiction, Ramayana, Bharatam, and Kandapuranam. They
are for the most parts comical, and abound in vulgar conceits and
voluptuous songs, gratifying to the vicious, but bearing no literary
value.
If any drama has seen the light, that mingles interest with instruction,
it is the excellent Manonmaniyam, by the late* lamented professor
Sundaram Pilli, M.A., published a few years ago. Eminently classical as
it is in diction, metre and matter, it is simply excellent in
characterisation and plot evolution, and will do well on the boards.
As a drama, what Manonmiyam is in verse, Rupavati is in prose. Though
it is no prise to say that both are free from the Pigeon-Tamil Which
obtins so much even among the Scholars of Southern India, who however
imbued with western knowledge and stirred up by Western example from
their wonted lethargy to a sense of national literature, only pander to
the tastes of low and the vulgar, our new-fledged writers would do well
to consult, the choice and chaste direction of these two dramas if they
would provide for their earnest readers opportunities of knowing what
the classical Tamil is, and what great purpose it can be made to serve.
Our Sastriar has in Rupavati pressed into service, whenever necessary,
the facilities* of Tamil diction, pure and unmixed, and given the
reading public not only an intellectual feat but so vivid portriture of
the ways and manners of princess and people of the age of the literary
*Witenagement in Madura that their imagination* cannot but be quickened,
exalted, and *ennobled. If he has succeeded in this as I verily believe
he has, Rupavati fulfils the mission it was intended to fulfil Into
tears of joy for, now appeared before them Sundara and Surupa stripped
of their disguise and clad in their usual apparel. Drunk with joy at the
sight of their missing son and dughter, they made haste to unite the
bands of those who were already united in their hearts; and Surasena,
convinced of the cruelty done to Nayavachana, ordered his release and
begged his pardon. Then Surasena invited Satguna, Virendra and others to
his metropolis and wished that a day should be fixed for the marriage.
An astrologer was consulted and the marriage of Ambujakshi, Suchila's
dughter, to Sukumara and Kanakamali to Nayavachana's son Chandramukha,
were proposed. Thus three marriages had tobe celebrated. But three is a
number of very bad omen, and so they arranged for the celebrations of
their marriage on the day, Friday, when happily fell the sixtieth-year
ceremony of the lureate of the Pandian Court, Vidhya Sagara, the guru
of Sundarananda and his associates. Thus was the objection obviated and
the occasion made jubiliant all around.

Rupavathi, the first fruit of the author is excellent in its own way. It
contins magnificent speeches which would repay perusal and the
obstacles in it and almost all poetical or have poetry in them.
Rupavathi, the heroine, is the only dughter of Surasena. Endowed by
nature with the gifts of body and mind, she falls in love with the only
son of Satguna. The parents of the lovers live only for their children,
and but for them their life and dominion are trills "light as air". The
angelic heroine is a perfect dissembler and is well acquainted with the
laws of fashion and etiquette. In Act I, Scene 2, her apparently
innocent questions 'will one swoon when he weaps? To continue the
narrative, and the skillful way in which she tries to draw out form her
companions an unvarnished tale of her lover's character and disposition,
and the sound rebuke administered to Kanakamali for her interference
while speaking amply testify to the statement. Further, she is a poetic
being and often speaks verses; she appreciates music and loves songs;
she exhibits the power of her tongue in the scene in which she brings
down Sundarananda to agree to their instance escape; she braves all
fatigues and hardships, being supported by the strength of her love; she
keeps a steady reserve in the court of Chola to avoid detection. Her
part, in this drama reminds us of the charming group of Shakespeare's
women such as Julia, Viola, Portia, Rosalind and Imogen who, under force
of circumstances assumed the disguise of male attire, What distinguishes
Rupavathi most is her learning, intelligence and fidelity in love.
Sundarananda, her lover, is a man of superior intelligence,
well-educated, and well-versed in the art of war. He is a Dhirodatta, to
use the expression of the Sanskrit dramatistik, and, in the language of
Suresena, he is an incarnation of meekness and gentleness, and is a poet
as well as a songster. His learning is manifest in his explanation of
the name Thiruvappanar and in his reference to Ahapporul ilakkanam (Act
III, scene 6). The most remarkable trit in his character, besides his
love to God, is his great attachment to his parents, of which he
thinkswith pious affection and tearful eyes, when they are doomed to
suffer the hardships of exile and when he is himself oppressed by the
pangs of love. Another noble trit in him is his gratefulness to his
benefactors.
Satguna, his father, is an incarnation of virtue. Full of piety and calm
in judgement, he is loved by his subjects as well as by his enemies. His
wife, Sundari, is a good match for him in every respect.
Suchila, his dutiful minister, is a subtle psychologist and sees through
Surasena. He is convinced that virtue will triumph independent of
extraneous aid. His ministerial tact is manifest in the scene where he
negotiates peace between Surasena and Virendra.
Surasena, father of the heroine, is rough, rude and ravengold. His
conquest has, as his dughter says, made him amibitious and hardened his
heart. He would yield to no persuation but force. It was only when his
forces failed that he conferred with his minister. The only redeeming
point in his character is that he is capable of remorse, and it is
probably due to this fact that he is not murdered, though he richly
deserved it, for his tyranny.
Jnanadipa is a sage indeed. He is very hospitable to strangers, and his
learned love is manifested in the scences where his disciple appears.
Unmindful of worldly honours, he rebukes prince and peasant alike for
their folly.
Vidya Sagara, the ocean of learning, is the Guru to the Prince and his
associates. All rever him for his wisdom, and quote his verses and sign
his songs most of all , Ambujakshi a clever songstress. The scene in
which he figures before Surasena and thunders forth his highly
eulogistic verses commemorating Surasena's late victory followed by his
learned interpretation thereof, is comical enough, when viewed in the
light of his question, 'Am I to be beaten?'. But this apparent
inconsistency illustrates a not uncommon occurrence among human beings,
viz., that when fear overtakes a man, his learning quits him and his
faculty of argumentation is at a stand.
Chandramukha and Sukamara, are the companions of the Prince to whom they
are firmly attached. The former relies upon experience and human effort
for success in life while the latter resigns everything to the will of
Providence. Hence these two characters represent two great schools of
Philosophy.
Imbujakshi and Kanakamali are the two associates of the Princess.
Former, a clever songstress, is shy by nature whereas Kanakamali is
petulmeddlesome. Further, the latter, thogh slightly superstitious,
often nils on the head when she has to judge of character.
The Lords appear in three of the scenes; and with Nayavachana, Surasena,
they lay their heads together to plot his death. The first Lord is
collected in his procedure and preaches aginst precipitancy in matters
moment. The second Lord, rather hasty, is wise on second thoughts, and
the third is a ruffian ready for immediate action and thirsting for
revenge.
Nayavachana isa man of policy. He always veneers his thoughts,f or
Surasena falls out with him, and he cannot be brow-beaten when he sees
tyranny previls. When the Lords are for the assassination of Surasena,
criticizes his judgement, and calmly refers the question to the decision
of Sudananda, of whose superior intelligence he has a high opinion.
Three DFreams are introduced in this play, two of them by Surasena,
Kamalavalli prognosticating evil and the third by the Nurse giving a
ray of the reappearance of Rupavati.
The Epilogue is spoken by Sundarananda. In it he discourses on human and
human love and rightly points out that domestic life, if rightly led, is
better and productive of better results than the life of an ascestic who
prefers seclusion and hates mankind.
To conclude, The plot of Rupavati, is so simple and dexterously
consistent and characterization so notable and happy, that they comine
to give the pleasure of the intellectual feasts given in it. I trust
that the entire play wright will continue to amuse and instruct the
reading public, year after year,with some fresh creations of his own,
and will reclim the dramatic love which is highly educative in its
value but much neglected by the Tamil people.
Coimbatore, March 1902 M.S. PURNALINGAM PILLAI.
--------------------

mukavuri

cēranāṭṭaracaṉākiya cūracēnavarmaṉ caṟkuṇavaḻuti yeṉṟa pāṇṭiyarācaṉit tirupparaṅ kuṉṟattiṟ
karukē naṭanta pōriṉ kaṇ veṉṟu maturiyampatiyit taṉakku irācatāṉiyākkik koṇṭu
pāṇṭiyaṉiyum avaṉ maṉiviyiṉiyum kāṭṭil turattiviṭṭu vaḻutimakaṉ cuntarānantaṉi
māttirañciṟiccāliyiliṭṭu aracupurintu vantāṉ. iṅṅaṉa mirukkiyil orunāṇmāli,
cuntarānantaṉ tāṉ veḷiyiṟ pōkum pōtu taṉṉik kākkumāṟu niyamikkappaṭṭa taṉ
ṟōḻarākiya cukumāra cantiramukarkaḷōṭu araṇ maṉik kaṭuttatōr pūṅkāvaṉañceṉṟu taṉatu
peṟṟōrkkuṟṟa katiyiṉiyeṇṇi ēṅkiṉāṉ.
avvamayattu cēraṉ makaḷākiya rūpāvati taṉṟōḻimārākiya ampujāṭci kanakamālikaḷōṭu
ulāvic celpavaḷ cuntarānantaṉik kaṇṇuṟṟuk kātalkoṇṭāḷ. iṅṅaṉañ cuntarānantaṉmītu kātal
koṇṭa rūpāvati vīṭṭiṟkēkit taṉ uḷḷaṅkoḷḷi koṇṭa talivaṉi niṉintu niṉintu uruki
āṟṟātu nāṭōṟum pūñcōlikkuc ceṉṟu taṉ kātala ṉit taricittuc ciṟituciṟitu āṟā
niṟpāḷ. ivvāṟē cuntarānantaṉum rūpāvatiyiṉmītu meynnēcamuṟṟu ayarvāṉāyiṉāṉ.
itaṟkiṭiyiṟ caṟkuṇavaḻutiyum avaṉ maṉiviyum kāṭṭil oru vēṭaṉutaviyāṉ ñānatīpa
muṉivaratu āciramamaṭintu muṉivarākkiṉippaṭi avviṭam viṭṭup puṟappaṭṭuccōṇāṭu
ceṉṟu, tañcimānakaril vīrēntira cōḻaṉ araṇmaṉiyil vativārāyiṉār. cūracēnavarmaṉō
vaḻutiyit turattiyatu mutaṟ kuṭikaḷiṭam koṭiyaṉeṉap pērpeṟṟu veṟukkap paṭṭāṉ.
atuvumaṉṟi avaṉukku naṇpaṉāyirunta nayavacanaṉuñ ciṟcila kaṉavāṉkaḷōṭu varmaṉit
tolikkumvaḻi nāṭuvaṉāyiṉāṉ.
maṟṟippaṭi rūpāvati cuntarānantaṉ ākiya ivviruvaruṭiya kātalum uriyāṭṭamilliyāyiṉum
vaḷarpiṟi pōṉ mutirāniṉṟatu. āyiṉuñ cuntarānantaṉ maṭṭum iṭiyiṭiyil taṉatu
peṟṟōri yeṇṇi varuntuvāṉ. ippaṭiyiruntu varunāḷil, tāṉ kāṭṭil turattiya
caṟkuṇavaḻutiyum avaṉ maṉiviyum cōṇāṭṭiliruppatit taṉṉoṟṟaṟāl uṇarnta cūracēkavarmaṉ
'pāṇṭiyaṉuñ cōḻaṉun taṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttu varuvārkaḷāyiṉ tāṉ ciṟiyiliṭṭirukkum
vaḻutimakaṉ cuntarānantaṉik koṉṟuviṭuvatu eṉṟu taṉakkuḷḷē tīrmāṉittukkoṇṭāṉ.
ivvaṇṇam ivaṉ tīrmāṉittati uṇarnta ivaṉ makaḷ rūpāvati taṉ kātalaṉākiya cuntarānantaṉukkut
taṉ tantiyiṉ antaraṅka eṇṇattiyum cuntarānantaṉ taṉṉi vantu maṟunāḷiraviṟ kāṇavēṇṭu
meṉṉun taṉ viruppattiyum oru kaṭitattil varintu piṉṉart tāṉ taṉatu nāyakaṉirukkuñ
ciṟiccāliyiṉ irakaciya vaḻip pūṭṭiṉ tiṟavukōliyeṭuttu an nirupattiṉkaṇ vittuc
curuṭṭit taṉ talivaṉatu ciṟiccālikkuḷ eṟintāḷ. ataṉ piṟaku cuntarānantaṉ
kaṭitaṅkaṇṭu ivvāṟu ceytavaḷ rūpāvatiyēyeṉṟu teḷintu piṟṟināḷiravil veḷiyiṟ
pōntu taṉ taliviyiṉ āṇippirakāram avaḷik kāṇpāṉ viḻintu niṟpuḻi, rūpāvati
āṇvēṭam pūṇṭuvantu taṉatu arumit talivaṉic cantittu avaṉōṭu vātāṭik kaṭiciyil
avaṉi uṭaṉpōkkiṟku iṇakkiyapiṉ, iruvaruñ cērntu maturimā nakariviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭu
vikaṟip pōḻtiṉ ñānatīparatu ācciramamuṟṟuc caṟṟiḷippāṟiya piṉ mīṭṭum puṟappaṭṭuc
cōṇāṭaṭintu cuntara curuṭar eṉṉum vēṟṟunāmaṅkaḷōṭu avviṭam iruppārāyiṉār.
itu niṟka. poḻutu pularntapiṉ ciṟi kāvalāḷar cuntarānantaṉik kāṇātu kavali kūrntu,
piṟaku 'avaṉ ciṟi matilēṟik kutit tōṭiṉaṉ ātaliṉ kollappaṭṭāṉ' eṉṟoru poyk kati
kaṟpittu varumaṉi nampumāṟu ceytaṉar. ataṉpiṉ varumaṉ antappurañ ceṉṟu āṇṭu
araṟṟāniṟkum kōmaḷa valli cevilikaḷiṉ mūlamāyt taṉmakaḷ rūpāvatiyik kāṇa villi
eṉpati uṇarntu eṅkun tēṭiyum akappaṭāmiyāl 'innāṭṭārē taṉmakaḷi iravileṭuttuc
ceṉṟu kolipurintaṉar' eṉṟu tuṇintu ūriluḷḷāriyellām oṟukkap pukuntāṉ. taṉatu
naṇpaṉākiya nayavacanaṉ mītum aiyamuṟṟu avaṉic ciṟippaṭuttiṉāṉ.
ivvāṟu varumaṉ ceyyum anitaṅkaḷip poṟukka māṭṭātu caṉaṅkaḷ varuntā niṟpac ciṉṉāṭ
kaḻiyalum, cōṇāṭṭiliruntu tantira tīraṉeṉṉum oru tūtuvaṉ pōntu pāṇṭi nāṭṭi
vaḻutiyiṉiṭam oppikkumāṟu ceppa, varumaṉ ataṟkiṇaṅkāṉāyp pōrvēṇṭit tāṉum taṉatu
karuvūrp patiyiṉkaṇuḷḷa pōr vīrari aḻittukkoṇṭu maturi varumāṟu āḷaṉuppiṉāṉ.
karuvūr ceṉṟa cēvakaṉ varumuṉ vaḻutiyuñ cōḻaṉum paṭiyoṭu vantu vikiyāṟṟiṉ
vaṭakarikkaṇ taṅkiṉar. cuntaraṉum oru viyūkattalivaṉāka vantiruntāṉ. ataṉ piṟaku
karuvūrc cēvakaṉ vantu 'avviṭattiṟ cēraṉ mantiri irācōpāyarukkum vaḻuti māmaṉār
vīramārttāṇṭan toṇṭirācarukkum yuttam naṭantu koṇṭirutta viṟpaṭivīrar varutaṟkillātu
pōyiṟṟu' eṉṟāṉ. itu cevicāttiya varumaṉ taṉ mantiriyākiya cucīlaṉi viḷittu
ālōcittu muṭiviṟ camātāṉañ ceyyumāṟu tāṉuñ cūcilaṉum mey kāppāḷaroṭu cōḻaṉ
pācaṟikkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ.
iṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ ceṉṟu cōḻaṉ, vaḻuti, mutalāyiṉārō ṭellām uṭaṉpaṭikkiyuṉ
nipantaṉikaḷik kuṟittup pēci muṭittapiṉ, vaḻutiyum varumaṉum taṅkaḷukku nēriṭṭa
makkaḷiḻaveṉṉum turpākkiyattiṉip paṟṟi iraṅkik koṇṭirukkum poḻutu, itukāṟum
māṟuvēṭam pūṇṭu cuntaraṉ curūpaṉ eṉṟu māṟupeyaruṅ koṇṭirunta cuntarānantaṉum
rūpāvatiyum tattamatu māṟuvēṭam nīttu uṇmi yuruvattoṭu veḷippaṭṭu muṟiyē
vaḻutiyiyum varumaṉiyum vaṇaṅki niṉṟārkaḷ! uṭaṉē āṇṭuk kuḻīi yiruntōrkaḷellām
ikkāṭciyiṉik kaṇṭu pērāṉantamuṟṟu em ālavāyp perumāṉatu aruḷi viyantu
tutittaṉar! appoḻutu yāvarum naṇparāyiṉār. varumaṉum taṉatu kāṇāmaṟpōṉa makaḷi mīṭṭum
peṟṟamiyāṟ kaḻipēruvakipūttu avviruvaratu maṇaviṉi muṭittaṟkuriya muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyat
toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. itaṉuṭaṉ nāṭakam muṭikiṉṟatu.
mutaṟpatipiṟ pōlātu ippatippiṟ pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ ayal pukaṭkēṟṟa colvaḻakkum
naṭivēṟupāṭum ciṟcila tiruttaṅkaḷum putiya ceyyuṭkaḷum amikkappaṭṭaṭuḷavāmāṟu
yāvaruṅ kaṇṭupōṟṟutaṟkuriyatu. ciṟcila ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ pāṭapētaṅkaḷuñ ceytirukkiṉṟaṉam.
palavakiyiṉum innāṭakam ematu nāṭakaviyalōṭu ottiyaṅkumāṟu kāṇka.
inta nāṭakamāṉatu maṉmata varuṭam āṭi mātam muppattiraṇṭāntēti viyāḻakkiḻamiyaṉṟiravu,
ceṉṉi vittiyāpimāṉa caṅkattār mūlamāy araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭatu. itaṉ kaṭkuṟṟaṅ kuṟikaḷ
irukkumāyiṟ pēraṟivuṭiyōr piriyattōṭum aviyiṟṟiṉit tirutti emmītu kirupi
purivārāka.
vi. kō. cū.

pāyiram

-------
vināyakar vaṇakkam
kaṭṭaḷikkalittuṟi
poṟpuṟu rūpā vatikāti taṉṉip pukaḻmikunta
naṟpaya ṉāṭaka māppin tamiḻi ṉavilutaṟkuc
ciṟpara vaḷḷaleñ cintā maṇiyaṉa cittitaruṅ
kaṟpaka yāṉi caraṇāra vintaṅ karutuvamē. (1)
cuppiramaṇiyar vaṇakkam
vañciviruttam
ulavuce luyartiṇik kuriyaṉik
kalavaṭi yavararuṭ kaṭaliṉik
kulavuṟ pulavayiṟ kumaraṉik
kaliyuṇar pulavaṉik karutuvām. (2)
nāmakaḷ vaṇakkam
kaliviruttam
kaṉṉirū pāvati kāti coṟṟiṭac
coṉṉayam poruṇayan tōṉṟu nūlpala
paṉṉuṟu pulavarnāp payilum pāmayil
poṉṉaṭit tāmari pōṟṟi vāḻkuvām. (3)
naṭarācar vaṇakkam
kalinilittuṟi
ulaka meṉṉumor nāṭaka cāliyi ṉuyirā
malaki lānaṭar taṅkaḷi* yatutina māṭṭi
yilaku cūttira tāraṉā miṟivaṉi yematu
taliva ṉānaṭa rācaṉit tāṇmalar paṇivām. (4)

nāṭaka pāttiraṅkaḷ

nāṭaka nikaḻviṭam
----------------
perumpāṉmi pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tali nakarākiya maturiyilum ataṟkaṭutta kāṭṭilum,
ciṟupāṉmi cōṇāṭṭut talinakarākiya tañciyilumām.

rūpāvati allatu kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa makaḷ

aṅkam - 1.
mutaṟ kaḷam.

------
iṭam:- oru pūñcōli
kālam:- māli
pāttiram :- cuntarānantaṉ.
cuntarānantaṉ: (taṉakkuḷ) pūkkaḷ malarntu vācaṉi vīcāniṉṟa iccōliyiṉkaṇ yāṉ vantu
ulāvi mantamāyaṭikkiṉṟa iḷan teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟiṉ cukatti yanupavippataṟku eṉ maṉam
oruppaṭukiṉṟa tilli. -eṉ ceyvēṉ! oru vārattiṟku muṉavariyiluṅ kaḷippuṟṟuc
cerukkittirinta eṉatu ēḻi neñcamē! yāṉ cantōṣappaṭuvati ēṉ taṭukkiṉṟāy? eṉmītu
ciṟitu karuṇi kūrntu eṉ vicaṉamellām nīṅkumpaṭi ceyvatu niṉak kaḻakēyaṉṟō?
(cukumāraṉuñ cantiramukaṉum varukiṉṟaṉar.)
antō! pāvi maṉamē! nī tuyarappaṭuvatumaṉṟi eṉṉiyum ēṉ varuttukiṉṟāy?
(avarkaḷ piṉṉē niṟkiṉṟaṉar.)
āṇṭu mayilka ḷāṭuvatiyum, kiḷikaḷ pēcik kulāvuvatiyum, nī pārttum uṉakku
iṉpamilliyō? -uṉṉiyē uyirt tuṇiyāyp peṟṟa eṉṉi nī ivvāṟu varuttukiṟpatu
tarmamākumō?
(pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)

(muṉam)
cukumāraṉ:- ē, cuntarānantā! iḵteṉṉi? mika viyappāyirukkiṉṟatē niṉ ceyal!
cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉa tarumiyāṉa cukumāraṉē! yāṉ eṉ ceyvēṉ? eṉmītu tavaṟoṉṟumilli.
eṉ maṉattuṉpam nīṅkiṟṟilli.
cantiramukaṉ:- nī yēḻunāḷāka ivvāṟutāṉ collukiṉṟāy!
cukumāraṉ:- āvatu āyiṟṟu. nām iṉi yeṉ ceyvatu? varuttamiṉṟik kālaṅ kaḻippataṉṟō
nalam?
cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉatu piriya nēcarkāḷ! eṉ maṉavaruttam nāḷukku nāḷ atikappaṭukiṉṟatē
yoḻiyak kuṟikiṉṟatilli. eṉṉuṭiya ūkkamellām eṅkēyō ōṭiviṭṭatu. eṉṉāṟ poṟukkak
kūṭavilli.
cantiramukaṉ:- nī kalvikaṟṟataṉ payaṉṟāṉeṉṉi? ettakiya tuṉpaṅkaḷ nēriṭṭālum,
avaṟṟiyellām poṟumiyuṭaṉ cakittuk koṇṭiruppataṉṟō mēṉmiyāṉatu. ellān terinta
uṉakku yāṉ collavum vēṇṭumō?
cukumāraṉ:- ellām valla kaṭavuḷ iṉpa vaṭivāṉavar; avar cakala kāriyaṅkaḷiyum maṉitaratu
naṉmikkākavē ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. ākiyāl avaratu aruḷiṉāl nikaḻnta icceyalkaḷellām
namakku naṉmiyiṉiyē payakkum eṉpati nī aṟiyāyō?
cuntarānantaṉ:- aṟivēṉ, aṟivēṉ.
cantiramukaṉ:- aṟintiruntum nī varuntuvatu nēritō? nīyuṉ peṟṟōri viṭṭup
pirintiruppataṉāṟ pātakam eṉṉa? nīyum avarkaḷum vevvēṟiṭaṅkaḷi liruntapōtilum, orē
yūrilē tāmē yirukkiṉṟīrkaḷ. aracaṉum nām niṉikkiṟapaṭi avvaḷavu koṭiyavaṉalla ṉeṉṟuṅ
kēḷvi. innāṭkaḷil oru nāṭṭaracaṉ iṉṉorunāṭ ṭaracaṉi veṉṟu avaṉic ciṟiyiliṭuvatu
vaḻakkāṟaṉṟō?
cuntarānantaṉ:- antō! antō! nīvirellām iṉṟu naṭanta camācārañ ciṟitun teriyātu
ivvāṟu collukiṉṟīrkaḷē! aiyakō! eṉceyvēṉ! eṉceyvēṉ! teyvamē!
cukumāraṉ:- veku āccariyamā yirukkiṉṟatē! eṅkaḷukkut teriyāta ceyti appaṭi
nikaḻntateṉṉi?
cuntarānantaṉ:- (pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)

[cuntarānantaṉ mūrccipōy viḻukiṉṟāṉ.]
iruvarum:- iḵteṉṉi! iḵteṉṉi! iḵteṉṉi putumi!!
[iruvarum mūrcci teḷivikkiṉṟaṉar.]
[pūñcōliyiṉ maṟṟoru cār, rūpāvati, kanakamāli,
ampujāṭci yivarkaḷ varukiṉṟaṉar.]
rūpāvati:- ampujāṭci! iṅkē camīpattilē yeṉṉavō aḻukik kural kēṭṭatē! aḵteṉṉeṉṟu
pōyp pārttuviṭṭu varuvamē?
ampujāṭci:- ēṉ, kanakamāli! rūpāvatitāṉ pārkkalām vāveṉṟu kūppiṭukiṟāḷē!
ellōrumāyp pōyttām pāppamē?
kanakamāli:- āṉāṟ cari. nīṅkaḷ iraṇṭu pērum kūppiṭukiṟapōtu nāṉ māṭṭēṉ eṉpēṉō,
nāṉ māṭṭēṉeṉṟāluntāṉ viṭuvīrkaḷō?
rūpāvatiyum ampujāṭciyum:- cari, pōkalām vā.
[ivarkaḷ pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
cukumāraṉ:- appā! cuntarānantā! ippaṭi eṉmēṟ cāyntukoḷ. ē cantiramukā! ippuṟam
vā, caṟṟuk kuḷircciyāyk kāṟṟu mēlē yaṭikkaṭṭum.
cantiramukaṉ:- ēṉ, cuntarānantā! uṉakku uṭampu eṉṉa ceykiṟatu?
[rūpāvati mutalāṉavar varukiṉṟaṉar.]
cuntarānantaṉ:- ammā! appā! ammā!! appā!! koñcam poṟuṅkaḷ.
rūpāvati:- iru, iru. iṅkētāṉ, iṅkētāṉ.
ampujāṭci:- atō, anta mātavi marattiṉkīḻ mūṉṟupēr uṭkārntirukkiṟārkaḷ. oruvaṉ
maṭiyiṟ pēraḻakuvāynta maṟṟoruvaṉ cāyntukoṇṭirukkiṟāṉ! pār.
kanakamāli:- āmām. nām irintu pēcak kūṭātu.
rūpāvati:- meḷḷac cattappaṭāmal nāminta mallikip pantarilēyē yoḷintuk koṇṭiruntu
avarkaḷ eṉṉa ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉṟupārppōm - nil, nil. eṉṉavō, pēcukiṟārkaḷ. kēṭpōm.
[ivarkaḷ oḷittup pārkkiṉṟaṉar.]
cantiramukaṉ:- eṉṉi itu? oru nāḷu millāmal iṉṟikku māttiram ippaṭi nī mūrcci
yaṭintu eṅkaḷi yellām varuttappaṭumpaṭi vittuviṭṭāy! ēṉappā, cuntarānantā!
cuntarānantaṉ:- (cukumāraṉ maṭiyi viṭṭu vilakikkoṇṭu) itik kuṟittu ñāpaka
mūṭṭātē; eṉṉi yoṉṟum ippoḻutu kēḷātē. nām iṉimēl, cukumārā! iṅkē tāmatañ
ceyyak kūṭātu.
cukumāraṉ:- antap payal ciṟikkāppāḷaṉ koñcam nēraṅkaḻittup pōṉāl atikamāyt
talikīḻāy viḻukiṟāṉ. 'aracaṉiṭattil teriyap paṭuttuvēṉ' eṉkiṟāṉ.
cantiramukaṉ:- āmām. cīkkiramāyp pōvōm. vāruṅkaḷ.
[cuntarānantaṉ, cukumāraṉ, cantiramukaṉ ivarkaḷ pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
rūpāvati:- ēṉ, kanakamāli! inta mūṉṟu pērum yār? nī yinta vūrilēyē irukkiṟavaḷ
tāṉē! uṉṉālē, ivarkaḷ iṉṉār eṉṟu collak kūṭumē!
ampujāṭci:- eṉṉik kēḷēṉ. nāṉ collukiṟēṉ. avaḷ inta vūrukku vantu iṉṉum
iraṇṭu varuṣaṅkūṭa ākavilli. avaḷuk kivarkaḷippaṟṟi eṉṉa teriyap pōkiṉṟaṉa!
kanakamāli:- ēṇṭiyammā! eṉakkum avarkaḷil oruttaṉit teriyum.
rūpāvati:- ēṉ ivvaḷavu pēccu? nīṅkaḷ iraṇṭu pērum inta vūrilēyē irukkiṟavarkaḷ
eṉṟu niṉittuc coṉṉēṉ. ippōtu yār coṉṉāltāṉ eṉṉa? ampujāṭci! nītāṉ col.
ampujāṭci:- illi, illi! avaḷukkut terinta maṭṭil avaḷē collaṭṭum; appuṟam
vēṇṭumeṉṟāl avaḷukkut teriyātatu iruntāl nāṉ collukiṟēṉ.
kanakamāli:- atoṉṟum illi. uṉakkuttāṉ naṉṟāyt teriyumē! collēṉ.
rūpāvati:- cari, irukkaṭṭum. ivarkaḷ yār? cīkkiram col. kēṭpōm.
ampujāṭci:- aḻakāy oru piḷḷi iṉṉoruttaṉ maṭiyilē uṭkārntu cāyntu
koṇṭiruntāṉē, avaṉ tāṉ munti irunta rājā caṟkuṇavaḻutiyiṉ piḷḷi.
kanakamāli:- avaṉittāṉ eṉakkum teriyum.
rūpāvati:- orutti collukiṟapōtu naṭuvilē pēcātē. uṉakkut terintāl appuṟam
col.
ampujāṭci:- avaṉ peyar cuntarānantaṉ. vilvittiyil veku keṭṭik kāraṉām, paṭippil
mikat tērntavaṉām. muṉṉirunta rājāvukku ivaṉ orē piḷḷi, ākiyiṉālē mika
arumiyāyk kaṇṇukkuk kaṇṇāy ivaṉi vaḷarttu vantār. ivaṉi maṭiyilē uṭkārttikkoṇ
ṭiruntāṉē avaṉ tāṉ eṉ takappaṉārōṭu kūṭap piṟanta attip piḷḷi. avaṉ peyari nāṉ
collappaṭātu!
kanakamāli:- avaṉukku vāḻkkippaṭap pōkiṟāyō? atu tāṉ pēr colla māṭṭēṉ eṉkiṟāy
pōlum!
rūpāvati:- yārukku vāḻkkippaṭap pōkiṟāḷ?
kanakamāli:- avaḷuṭiya atti piḷḷikkām. iṉṉum kalyāṇam kūṭa ākavilli. ataṟkuḷḷē pēr
collappaṭātu eṉkiṟāḷ!
rūpāvati:- ēṇṭi ampujāṭci! aviyellām irukkaṭṭum. nī aḻakāy oruvaṉ aracaṉ makaṉ
eṉṟu coṉṉāyē avaṉ aḻuvāṉēṉ?
ampujāṭci:- ellām uṉakkē terintirukkumē!
rūpāvati:- illi, niccayamāyc collukiṟēṉ. eṉakkoṉṟum teriyātu.
ampujāṭci:- āṉāṟ caritāṉ. oruvēḷi uṉakkut terintirukkumō veṉṟu niṉittēṉ.
uṅkaḷ takappaṉār intap pāṇṭiya rājyatti jeyittu intat tēcattu rājāviyum, rājā
peṇṭāṭṭiyiyum oru ciṟiyilēyum, inta rājāpiḷḷi, ippōtu pārttōmē, avaṉi
iṉṉoru ciṟiyilēyum aṭittu vittiruntār. anta rājāvum rāṇiyum inta vūrilēyē
irukkiṟataṉālē kalakaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu niṉittu avarkaḷ iraṇṭu peyariyum iṉṟik
kālitāṉ inta vūrukkaṭutta oru kāṭṭilē koṇṭupōyt turattiviṭṭu varumpaṭi uṅkaḷ
tantiyār uttaravu koṭuttārām. antappaṭiyē cēvakarkaḷum avarkaḷi aḻittukkoṇṭu
turatti viṭṭuvarak kāṭṭukkuppō yirukkiṟārkaḷām. intac camācārattik
kēṭṭuviṭṭuttāṉ cuntarānantaṉ aḻutatu. tāyār takappaṉār eṉṟāl avaṉukkup pirāṇaṉ!
eṉṉa ceyvaṉ?
rūpāvati:- ēṇṭi! mūrccipōy viḻuntāṉāmē! avarkaḷ pēcik koṇṭārkaḷē! aḵteṉṉi!
aḻutāl mūrcci pōkumō?
kanakamāli:- tāyār takappaṉārik kāṭṭut tuṣṭamirukaṅkaḷukku iriyākumpaṭi vayatāṉa
kālattilē turattiviṭṭa caṅkatiyik kātilē kēṭṭum mūrcci pōkātavar yār?
appērppaṭṭavar mikavum kaṉṉeñcarāyt tām irukkavēṇṭum.
ampujāṭci:- anta cukumāraṉum, cantiramukaṉum, pakkattilē illā tiruntāl,
cuntarānantaṉ, appāvi! varuttameṉpati iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyātavaṉ! veku avastippaṭṭup
pōyiruppāṉ. cuvāmi, avarkaḷic camīpatti lirukkumpaṭi ceytārē! atuvaṉṟō vicēṣam!
kanakamāli:- atellām irukkaṭṭum. appōtu māttiram akamuṭiyāṉ peyaric collappaṭā
teṉṟāyē! ippōtu māttiram collalāmō?
rūpāvati:- iṉṉum kalyāṇam ākavilliyē! coṉṉāl eṉṉa? kuṟṟamilli.
kanakamāli:- cantiramukaṉeṉṟu coṉṉāyē, avaṉ yār?
ampujāṭci:- avaṉ tāṉ nayavacakar eṉpavaruṭiya puttiraṉ. cuntarā nantaṉōṭukūṭap
paṭittavaṉ. ivarkaḷum, eṅkaḷ atti piḷḷiyum āka mūṉṟupeyarum vittiyācākarap
pulavariṭam kalvi kaṟṟuk koṇṭārkaḷām.
rūpāvati:- aḵtirukkaṭṭum. cuntarānantaṉip paṟṟi ivvūrār eṉṉa collik
koḷḷukiṟārkaḷ?
kanakamāli:- cuntarānantaṉā? makāputticāli! ūrār collukiṟateṉṉa? naṉṟāyt terinta
viṣayantāṉē!
rūpāvati:- ampujāṭci! kanakamāli māttiram eṉṉavō ippaṭic collukiṟāḷ. nāṉum
iraṇṭu mūṉṟu pēriṭattilē kēṭṭēṉ; avaṉ eṉṉavō veku tuṣṭaṉām. tāṉ tāṉ aracaṉ makaṉ
eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭu ūrmakkaḷi ellām piṭittukkoṇṭu aṭittu varuttappaṭuttuvāṉām.
ippaṭi ellām collik koḷḷukiṟārkaḷē!
ampujāṭci:- cuntarānantaṉiyuñ colli maṟṟip peyariyum collukiṟatā? veku
naṉṟāyirukkiṟatu! avaṉā tuṣṭaṉām? appaṭic collukiṟavarkaḷ nākkup puḻuttuttāṉ
pōkum. parama cīva kāruṇṇiya ṉeṉṟāl avaṉukkuttāṉ takum.
kanakamāli:- appaṭi irukka, aḻaku aḻaku, yār coṉṉatō teriyavilli!
ampujāṭci:- intappaṭi coṉṉāltāṉ ippōtu vantirukkira rājā cūracēnavarmar taṅkaḷ
pērilē piriyamāyiruppāreṉṟu niṉittārkaḷō! avarkaḷum naṉṟāyc coṉṉārkaḷ, rājāvum
naṉṟāyk kēṭṭukkoṇṭār! vīṭṭilē irukkiṟapōtē vitivantu cūḻntatupōla, teyvamē eṉṟu
irukkiṟavaṉ taliyilē naṉṟāyp paḻiyip pōṭṭārkaḷ! cuvāmi irukkiṟār ellāvaṟṟiṟkum!
nām colliyā ākappōkiṟatu?
kanakamāli:- ulivāyi mūṭalām, ūrvāyi mūṭalāmō? collukiṟavarkaḷ colliviṭṭup
pōkaṭṭum!
ampujāṭci:- yār eṉṉa coṉṉapōtilum avaṉip paṟṟi nammuṭiya eṇṇam eṉṉavō māṟap
pōkiṟatilli.
rūpāvati:- eṉṉavō? ammā! ūrār coṉṉatic coṉṉēṉ. avaṉ nallavaṉāka iruntāl ellām
nallatu tāṉē!
kanakamāli:- eṉakkut terintamaṭṭil aḻaku, putti, cakti, kuṇam ivaṟṟilellām
avaṉukkuc camāṉamāṉavarkaḷ illi eṉṟē collalām.
rūpāvati:- cari, aḵtu appaṭi irukkaṭṭum, ampujāṭci ētāvatu oru pāṭṭup pāṭu
kēṭpōm. naṉṟāy mantamārutam vīcukiṟatu. eṅkē! cukamāyp pāṭu, kēṭpōm.
ampujāṭci:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

rūpāvati:- camayattuk kēṟṟa pāṭṭum pāṭṭukkēṟṟa kuralum irākamum tāḷamum ellām
naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kanakamāli:- avaḷukkup pāṭukiṟataṟkuk kēṭpāṉēṉ? jaṉmāntarattilē puṇṇiyam paṇṇi
irukkiṉṟāḷ! atu tāṉ teyvam nalla kuralāyk koṭuttirukkiṟatu!
ampujāṭci:- iṉṟikku eṉṟu eṉṉavō koñcam toṇṭi kaṭṭik koṇṭirukkiṟatu,
allāmaṟpōṉāl iṉṉum caṟṟu naṉṟāyp pāṭi irukkalām.
rūpāvati:- pāṭṭuppāṭukiṟavarkaḷellārum aḵtu eṉṉiyō teriyavilli, toṇṭi
kaṭṭikkoṇṭiruntālum cari illāmaṟ pōṉālum cari, ippaṭittāṉ collukiṟārkaḷ.
kanakamāli:- atu avarkaḷukku vaḻakkamāyp pōyviṭṭatu. ataṟku avarkaḷ eṉṉa ceyvārkaḷ?
ampujāṭci:- cari. nām ippaṭip pēcikkoṇṭiruntāl, namakku nāḻikiyāka villi?
astamittup pōyiṟṟu. akattiṟkup pōka vēṇṭāmā?
kanakamāli:- āmām, cūriyaṉ appōtē malivāyilē viḻuntu viṭṭāṉē. cī, cī, iṉimēl
irukkappaṭātu. ammā, eṅkaḷappā kōpittuk koḷvār.
rūpāvati:- ām, eṉakkum nēramāyviṭṭatu.
[yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
-------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 1 / iraṇṭāṅkaḷam

iṭam:- kāṭu
kālam:- naṇpakal
pāttiraṅkaḷ:- caṟkuṇaṉ, cuntari
caṟkuṇaṉ:- iṉṉum koñcantūram pōṉāl oru vēlamara mirukkiṉṟatu, ataṉ niḻalil
taṅkuvōm. ciṟitu virivāy vā, aiyō! uṉ pātaṅkaḷ tariyi mitittaṟiyāvē! uṉakku
veyyileṉpatē teriyātē! eṉ ceyvēṉ! ituvum eṉ talivitiyō? ā! teyvamē!
cuntari:- teyvantāṉ eṉṉaceyyum? nāmtām eṉṉa ceyvōm? koñcat tūrattiṟ kappāl
vēlamaram oṉṟu irukkiṟateṉṟīrkaḷē! aḵteppaṭi uṅkaḷukkut teriyum?
caṟkuṇaṉ:_ antō! uṉṉik kippiṭitta intappāvi aracaṉāyirunta kālattiṟ palataram itē
kāṭṭil vēṭṭiyāṭa vantirukkiṉṟāṉ!
cuntar:- veyyilō akōramāyk koḷuttukiṉṟatu! ippaṭiyē aḻutu koṇṭu pōvōmāṉāl
uṭampu kaḷittup pōyviṭum. appuṟam naṭakkamuṭiyātu.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- aiyō! eṉkaṇṇē! uṉakku irukkiṟa aṟivu eṉakkilliyē! eṉ ceyvēṉ!
cuntari:- nammip piramā avastippaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟē niṉittu mutalil irājya paripālaṉatti
yellām koṭuttu ippōtu avaṟṟi yellām piṭuṅkik koṇṭu tuṣṭa mirukaṅkaḷukku
iriyākumpaṭi paṭittuviṭṭāṉ! kāṭō makā kaṭōramā yirukkiṟatu! veyyilō iṉṟikku
eṉṟu makōkkiramā yirukkiṟatu! tiṉamum irunta tiriyamum iṉṟu cōtaṉi ceytatupōlak
ki viṭṭup pōyviṭṭatu! pūrva jaṉmattil nām eṉṉa pāvam ceytōmō avaṟṟiṉ payaṉi ellām
ippōtu aṉupavikkumpaṭi nēriṭṭu viṭṭatu! vaḷappamuḷḷa nāṭṭi iḻantōm! veku
nērttiyāṉa maturi nakari iḻantōm! alaṅkāramāṉa araṇmaṉiyi iḻantōm! uṭiyi iḻantōm!
poruḷi iḻantōm! mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar taricaṉatti iḻantōm! 'cuntarēcuvarar' eṉṟa
uṭaṉē ñāpakam varukiṟatu. ellāp pākkiyattilum mēlāṉa nammuṭiya arumic cuntarā
nantaṉiyum iḻantōm! iḻantōm!! teyvamē! teyvamē!! cuntarānantā! cuntarānantā!! uṉṉi
iḻantapiṟakum uyir tarippēṉō?
[cuntari mūrccipōy vīḻkiṉṟaṉaḷ.]
caṟkuṇaṉ:- aiyō! eṉṉa ceyvēṉ! nī ciṟitu tiriyamā yirukkiṟāyē eṉṟu niṉittēṉ.
nīyu mivvāṟu mūrcci pōyviṭṭāyē! ivvaṉāntarattiṟ koñcam jalam, mayakkam tirumpumpaṭi
uṉ mukattil teḷikkalām eṉṟālum jalamilliyē! eṅkēyāvatu cuṟṟip pārttukkoṇṭu
varuvōm eṉṟālum uṉṉit taṉiyāy viṭṭuviṭṭup pōṉāṟ puliyaṭittukkoṇṭu pōy viṭṭā
leṉceyvēṉ! kaṭināḷil, nāṭu, nakar ellāvaṟṟiyum tuṟantēṉ! arumputalvaṉi yiḻantu,
puttiracōka muṟṟēṉ! kaṭiciyil eṉ uyirt tuṇiviyākiya cuntariyiyum iḻakka
vēṇṭumō! vēṇṭumō!! cuntarī! cuntarī!! eṉṉi ippaṭi ēkāṅkiyāy viṭṭup pirital
niyāyamō?

(muṉam) 'aiyā' eṉṟa captam kēṭṭēṉ. ā! yārō pārppēṉ.
[oru vēṭaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
vēṭaṉ:- ēṉ aiyā! ippaṭi yaḷukiṟiya? uṅkaḷukku - ākākākā! yārō ammā! eṉṉa
mayakkamō? itō taṇṇiyirukkutu. teḷiyuṅka mukattilē.
[vaḻutiyār taṇṇīr teḷikkiṉṟār.]
caṟkuṇaṉ:- koñcam ammāvukku veyyil maṟivāy nil appā!
[vēṭaṉ avvāṟu niṟṟalum, vaḻutiyār tūkkimēlē cāyttuk koḷkiṉṟār; cuntari
mūrcci teḷikiṉṟāḷ.]
cuntari:- hā! hu!
caṟkuṇaṉ:- eṉ pirāṇa nāyakiyē! eṉakku tiriyañ coṉṉāyē! nī ivvāṟu mūrccipōy
eṉṉit tuṉpappaṭuttutal cariyākumō? nī yappaṭiyē uyir tuṟantiruppiyāyiṉ yāṉtāṉ
piḻippēṉō? iviyellām emperumāṉ viḷiyāṭē yallavō? "avaṉiṉṟi yōraṇuvum
aciyātu" eṉṟa periyōr vacaṉam poyttup pōkumō? eṉmuttē! eṉmītu cāyntu koṇṭu
caṟṟu iḷippāṟuvāy!
vēṭaṉ:- aiyā! iṅkē pulikaraṭi mutaliya pirāṇikaḷ cuttittiriyumē iṅkē yirukkak
kūṭātē-
caṟkuṇaṉ:- nāṅkaḷ cākavēṇṭiyatum kaṭavuḷukku iṣṭamāṉāl atuvum naṭantē tīrum. ataṟkāka
nām payappaṭuvatēṉ?
cuntari:- appaṭiyilli. nām nammālāṉa muyaṟcikaḷic ceytāl naṭakkiṟapaṭi naṭakkaṭṭum.
kaṭavuḷ nammuṭiya ceyalkaḷ mūlamākavētāṉ namakku naṉmi yiṉiyun tīmiyiṉiyum
viḷikkiṉṟār. ākiyāl nām oṉṟuñ ceyyātiruntāl namakku naṉmi viḷivateppaṭi?
caṟkuṇaṉ:- eṉatarumit taliviyē! nī colvaṉa vellām niyāyantām. nām ippoḻutu taṅki
yiruppataṟku iṭam eṅkē?
vēṭaṉ:- aiyā! nīṅka atukku yōcaṉi ceyyavēṇṭām. itukkarukālē iraṇṭu nāḷiyavaḷit
tūrattilē oru cāmiyār cāvaṭiyirukkutu. ippō uṅkaḷik kūṭṭip pōy antac cāmiyār
maṭattilē viṭṭuppōṭṭu appuṟam vīṭṭukkup pōṟēṉ.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- antac cāmiyār peyareṉṉa?
vēṭaṉ:- atu eṉakkut teriyātu cāmi. avar cāvaṭi māttiran teriyum. varukiṟatiṇṭā
vāṅka.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- koñcam poṟuttukkoḷ, appā! varukiṟōm:- ēṉ, puṟappaṭuvamā?
cuntari:- iṉṉum evvaḷavu tūram irukkum appā! antac cāvaṭi?
vēṭaṉ:- itō! kiṭṭattilē tāṉ ammā!
cuntari:- āṉāṟ cari.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- mella! mella! eṉ kiyip piṭittukkoḷ.
vēṭaṉ:- nāṉ muṉṉukkup pōṟēṉ. piṉṉukku nīṅka metuvā vāṅka.
[yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
-------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 1 / mūṉṟāṅkaḷam

iṭam:= kāṭṭil muṉivarācciramam
kālam:- māli
pāttiraṅkaḷ:- ñāṉatīpar, āṉmanātar
ñāṉatīpar:- appā! ātmanātā! ceyyavēṇṭiya kāriyattiyellāñ ceytu viṭṭāyā?
civañānapōta cūttiraṅkaḷi nēṟṟuc ciravaṇam paṇṇiṉāyē! iṉṟikku avaṟṟi ellām maṉaṉam
paṇṇiviṭṭāyā? mēṟ pōkalāmā?
āṉmanātar:- ākñāppirakāram, cakala kāriyaṅkaḷiyuñ campūrṇappaṭutti viṭṭēṉ. ciravaṇam
paṇṇiṉa cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ cila maṉattil avanāhappaṭavilli. ākiyāl avi muḻuvatiyum maṉaṉam
paṇṇat taṭippaṭṭatu. ākiyā leṉṉi kṣamittu eṉmītu mīṭṭuṅ kirupi kūravēṇṭum.
ñānatīpar:- antap pirakāramē, uṉakku maṟupaṭiyum maṉattiṟ paṭumpaṭi collukiṉṟēṉ.
itaṟku muṉṉē śrīharananta civācāriyar paḻukkap paḻukka neruppilē kāynta iruppu
mukkāliyilē vīṟṟiruntu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiya civaparatva nirūpaṇakalōka pañcakam
paṭaṉamāyiṟṟā?
āṉmanātar:- tiruvuḷap pirakāram, paṭaṉamāyiṟṟu. kaṭṭaḷiyaḷittāṟ collukiṉṟēṉ.
[caṟkuṇaṉ, cuntari, vēṭaṉ ivarkaḷ viḷikkiṉṟaṉar.]
ñānatīpar:- atirukkaṭṭum. yārō vācalilvantu 'cāmī! cāmī!' eṉṟu kūppiṭukiṟatu pōlat
tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ateṉṉaveṉṟu pōyppārttu vā.
[āṉmanātar veḷiyē pōy varukiṉṟār.]
āṉmanātar:- yārō iruvar, stirīpumāṉkaḷ, veyyilāṟ kaḷittavar,cevviya mēṉiyar, ciṟanta
nōkkiṉar, vāṭiya mukattiṉar, aḻuta kaṇṇiṉar, vantuḷar. avarkaḷ camīpattil oru vēṭaṉum
niṟkiṉṟāṉ.
ñāṉatīpar:- avviruvarum oruvēḷi namatu āciramattil taṅkalām eṉṟu vantārkaḷ pōlum!
nīpōy avarkaḷi yiṅkē yiṭṭukkoṇṭu vantu viṭṭuviṭṭu avarkaḷukku ētēṉum
pōjaṉattiṟku ēṟpāṭu cey.
āṉmanātar:- appaṭiyē ceykiṉṟēṉ.
[pōkiṉṟār.]
(vēṭaṉoḻiya maṟṟa yāvarum varukiṉṟaṉar.)
[caṟkuṇaṉuñ cuntariyum oruṅkē muṉivari vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.]
ñānatīpar:- tīrkkāyuṣmāṉ pava!- tīrkka cumaṅkalīpava!-nīṅkaḷiruvarum rāja lakṣaṇaṅkaḷōṭu
kūṭiṉavarkaḷā yirukkiṉṟīrkaḷē! uṅkaḷukku intak kati campavippāṉēṉ?
caṟkuṇaṉ:- cuvāmikāḷ! aṭiyēṉ pāṇṭiya tēcat taracaṉāvēṉ. ālāciyakṣēttira vāciyā
yiruntēṉ. caṟkuṇap peyariṉēṉ. ivaḷ eḷiyēṉatu pāriyi yāvāḷ. cuntarip peyariṉāḷ.
toṇṭi nāṭṭaracaṉ vīramārttāṇṭaṉuṭiya makaḷ. cēra tēcātipati cūracēṉavarmaṉāṟ
ceyikkappaṭṭu ivvāraṇiyattiṟkut tuṣṭa mirukaṅkaḷukku iriyākumpaṭi turattappaṭṭōm.
vaḻiyiloru vēṭaṉic cantittōm. avaṉatu utaviyāl tēvarīratu āciramattiṟku vantu
cērntōm.
ñānatīpar:- icceyti umatu māmaṉārākiya toṇṭi nāṭṭaracaṉukkut teriyātō?
caṟkuṇaṉ:- teriyātu. veṭkampaṟṟi avariṭam utavi kēṭkavilli. yāṉē pōrkkaḷam ceṉṟu
yuttattiṟ ṟolivuṇṭēṉ.
ñānatīpar:- ippōtu ikkati ataṉiṉum veṭkak kēṭāyiṟṟē! ippaṭiyirukkalāmā?
caṟkuṇaṉ:- ām. eṉ ceyvatu?
ñānatīpar:- ippōtu umakku toṇṭi nāṭṭiṟkup pōy vatiya iṣṭamā? allatu cōḻa
nāṭṭiṟkup pōy vatiya iṣṭamā? eṉakkut taṟkālattil rājarīkañceytu varukiṉṟa vīrēntira
cōḻaṉit teriyum.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- tēvarīratu iṣṭa meppaṭiyō appaṭiyē naṭakkak kāttirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ñānatīpar:- āṉāl uṅkaḷiruvariyum cōḻa nāṭṭiṟku aṉuppuvatā yuttēcittirukkiṟēṉ.
antap pirakāramē ceyvīrkaḷeṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- ā, appaṭiyē yākaṭṭum.
ñānatīpar:- appā! ātmanātā!
āṉmanātar:- itō! vantuviṭṭēṉ.
[āṉmanātar varukiṉṟār.]
ñānatīpar:- ivarkaḷiruvarum taṅkiyiruppataṟku vacatiyāṉa ōriṭam pārttu ivarkaḷukkuk
koṭu. ivarkaḷukkup pōjaṉa mutalāṉavikaḷum ceytu vi.
āṉmanātar:- ākñāp pirakāram ceykiṟēṉ.
caṟkuṇaṉ:- ē, pirāṇacuntari! cākṣāt mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar kirupi nampēriṟ
paripūraṇamā yirukkiṉṟa teṉpataṟkuc cantēkamēyilli. pār! nammuṭiya jaṉmāntara
pāvattiṉāl yām aṉupavikkumpaṭi nēriṭṭa tuṉpaṅkaḷi yellām nīkkuvataṟkup
paramapatiyākiya civaperumāṉār, nī mūrccipōy viḻuntapōtu oru vēṭaṉik koṇarntu
viṭṭār! piṟaku taṅkuvataṟku mikavum vacatiyāṉa oru muṉipuṅkavaruṭiya āciramattik
koṇarntu viṭṭār! nām ittaṉi nāḷāka irājya paripālanam paṇṇiyum inta muṉicirēṣṭar
ituvarikkum inta vaṉattil iruppatu namakkut teriyātiruntatē! inta ñāṉatīparuṭiya
ācirvātamum namakkuk kiṭikkumpaṭiyum antac cantiracēkararē ceytirukkiṉṟār.
tiruvaruṭ kaṟpaka teyvacikāmaṇi (pāṭukiṉṟāṉ.

cuntari:- ataṟkum aiyamuṇṭō? kaṭampavanēcar karuṇiyiṉār cakalamum
naṉmiyāy māṟak kaṭavaṉa. muṉivaruṭiya karuṇi yeṉṉē!
[yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
--------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 1 / nāṉkāṅkaḷam

iṭam:- pāṇṭiyavaraṇmaṉi
kālam:- kāli
pāttiraṅkaḷ:- cūracēnaṉ, cucīlaṉ
cūracēnaṉ:- nām intap pāṇṭiya nāṭṭik kavarntu nām maturiyampatiyi namatu rācatāṉi
yākkikkoṇṭu nām navarattiṉaṅkaḷā liḻikkappeṟṟa poṟpīṭattiṉmītu vīṟṟiruntu nām aracu
purivatu ittēcattār yāvarkkum cantōṣantāṉē? ēṉ? cucīlarē?
cucīlaṉ:- tāṅkaḷ caṟkuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉi veṉṟa makāvīrar! taṅkaḷ irāja rīkattukkuk kuṟi
colluvāru muḷarō? innakaruḷār yāvarum tāṅkaḷ inta maturāpuriyiyē talinakarākkik
koṇṭō meṉṟu paṟiyaṟivitta aṟṟināḷ aṭinta āṉantattiṟ kōraḷavu muṇṭō?
cūracēnaṉ:- eṉṉa! iṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉiyum avaṉ maṉiviyiyuṅ kāṭṭil
turattiviṭṭuvarumpaṭi pōṉa cēvakarkaḷ varavilli!
cucīlaṉ:- cēvakarkaḷ varukiṟa camayantāṉ. itō vantu viṭuvārkaḷ.
cūracēnaṉ:- nām avarkaḷik kāṭṭil turattiviṭṭu varumpaṭi uttaravu koṭutta camācāram
ūrārukkut teriyumō?
cucīlaṉ:- teriyumpōlattāṉ tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
cūracēnaṉ:- avarkaḷukku eppaṭi terintirukkak kūṭum?
cucīlaṉ:- eppaṭit terintatō? namakkeṉṉa? payamā? aḵtoṉṟu milliyē.
cūracēnaṉ:- nām payattiṟkākak kēṭkavilli. teriyakkūṭiya vaḻi yeppaṭiyōveṉṟu
terintukoḷḷat tām kēṭṭōm.
cucīlaṉ:- jaṉaṅkaḷ; ēḻikkuṭikaḷ! avarkaḷ mēlum nallavarkaḷ.
cūracēnaṉ:- cari, ataṟkeṉṉa ippōtu? nayavacanareṅkē ituvariyiluṅ kāṇōm.
cucīlaṉ:- itō vantuviṭṭārē! pāruṅkaḷ.
[nayavacanaṉ varukiṉṟār.]
cūracēnaṉ:- ō! nayavacanarē! umakku vayatu nūṟu! nāṅkaḷ niṉikkavum nīrum vantīrē!
nayavacanaṉ:- makārājā avarkaḷ camūkattiṟku aṭiyēṉ vantaṉam.
cūracēnaṉ:- nirampa cantōṣam. ippaṭiyuṭkārum.
nayavacanaṉ:- ākñippaṭiyē!
cūracēnaṉ:- nām tōṟkaṭittōmē, antap pāṇṭiyaṉ avaṉ puttiraṉ. avaṉ pēreṉṉa?
cuntarānantaṉ, ām cuntarānantaṉ. avaṉukkut tōḻarākavum veḷiyilē pōkumpōtu
kāvalāḷarākavum, iraṇṭupēri niyamittōmē, avarkaḷil oruttaṉ umatu kumāraṉ -
maṟṟoruvaṉ - cucīlarvakiyō? caritāṉ, caritāṉ. eṉṉattiṟkākak kēṭṭēṉeṉṟāl
teriyātavarkaḷāyiruntāl oru vēḷi avaṉukku uṭaṉpāṭāyiruntu ētēṉum viparītañ
ceyyakkūṭum; ataṟkākattāṉ.
nayavacanaṉ:- (taṉakkuḷ) evvaḷavu nayavañcakaṉ! kapaṭaṉ! cūtukkāraṉ! koliñaṉ!
cucīlaṉ:- nām yāvaṟṟiluṅ kavaṉamāyiruppatu niyāyantāṉē?
cūracēnaṉ:- ēṉ aiyā - cucīlarē! ummuṭiya vaki maṉuṣaṉ peyareṉṉa?
cucīlaṉ:- evaṉic collukiṉṟīrkaḷ?
cūracēnaṉ:- avaṉtāṉ ciṟiccāliyilē kiṭakkiṉṟāṉē! avaṉ tōḻaṉ.
cucīlaṉ:- eṉṉuṭiya tamakki piḷḷitāṉ; avaṉ peyar cukumāraṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:- inta ūrilē nalla tamiḻ vittuvāṉkaḷ irukkiṉṟārkaḷā?
cucīlaṉ:- ēṉ ataṟkeṉṉa? camastāṉa cūriyum makā vittuvāṉumākiya vittiyācākarap pulavar
irukkiṉṟār.
cūracēnaṉ:- nām pāṇṭiyaṉōṭu purinta yuttattil aṭinta veṟṟiyip paṟṟik
kavipāṭumpaṭi avarkku uttaravu koṭukkavēṇṭum. appaṭiyallāviṭṭāl nammuṭiya karuvūrc
camastāṉa vitvāṉ vaṇṇak kaḷañciyap pulavarukku uttaravu koṭuppōm.
cucīlaṉ:- ākaṭṭum. antappaṭiyē vittiyācākarap pulavariṭan taṅkaḷuṭiya uttaraviṉit
terivikkiṉṟēṉ.
[cēvakaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
cēvakaṉ:- makārācā avuka camūkattilē kumpiṭṟēṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:- aṭē! cēvakā! ēṉ ivvaḷavu tāmatam?
cēvakaṉ:- makārācā! avukaḷikkūṭṭi veku tolipōy, vantavaḷi teriyāmē,
naṭukkāṭṭilē, veyyil koḷuttak koḷuttak koṇṭu viṭṭiṭṭu vantēṉ. atutāṉ
nēramāyiṟiccu.
cūracēnaṉ:- nēṟṟē pōṉāyēṭā?
cēvakaṉ:- āmām makārācā! nāṉ tirumpi vāriyilē iruṭṭip pōyiṟiccu. atutāṉ ippa
vanteṉ. illāṭṭi appavē vantiruppēṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:- nī avarkaḷik kāṭṭilē turattiviṭṭu varukiṟapōtu avarkaḷ eṉṉa
pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ? uḷḷatic collavēṇṭum. illāviṭil uṉ taliyi inta vāḷiṉāl
tuṇṭittup pōṭuvēṉ.
[vāḷiyōccukiṉṟāṉ.]
cēvakaṉ:- makārācāvē! nāṉ avukaḷik kāṭṭilē viṭṭavuṭaṉē avuka kōoṇṭu vāyviṭṭuk
katṉāka. anta vēḷiyilē puli karaṭi vantu tiriyumpōlat tōṇiccu. kāṭō veku
payammāyirunticcu. ākiyiṉālē, eṅkē puli eṉṉiyum aṭiccukkiṭṭu pōyirumō eṇṭu
payantu nāṉ eṉṉālē muṭiñcamaṭṭum veku vēkamā ōṭiyāntiṭṭēṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:- avarkaḷ eṉṉaṭā pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭāl ivaṟṟiyellām uṉṉi yār
collac coṉṉārkaḷ? ēṇṭā? avarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭatic collukiṉṟāyā eṉṉa ippōtu?
cēvakaṉ:- makārācāvē! nīṅkaḷ eṉṉik koṇṭāluñ cari viṭṭāluñ cari. nāṉ uḷḷatittāṉ
colṟēṉ. poy collalli. avuka vimmi vimmiyaḷutākaḷē yoḷiya vēṟē etuvum pēcalli.
cūracēnaṉ:- nijantāṉā?
cēvakaṉ:- āmām. makārācā! nicantāṉ.
cucīlaṉ:- makārājā avarkaḷukkaṉṟō nāṉ colluvatu?
cūracēnaṉ:- eṉṉa collukiṟīr? cucīlarē! collum, kēṭpōm.
cucīlaṉ:- avarkaḷ naṭukkāṭṭiṟ pōṉa uṭaṉē avarkaḷukku aḻattōṉṟumē tavira vēṟoṉṟum
pēcat tōṉṟātu. avarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ aṭinta tuṉpaṅkaḷi niṉikkumpōtellām taṅkaḷiaṟiyāmalē
aḻuvārkaḷ. aḻutu, āṟi yōynta piṟaku tāṉ etāvatu pēcukiṟataṟkum, yōcaṉi
ceyvataṟkum tōṉṟum; ataṟku muṉṉē vēṟoṉṟum muṭiyātu. inta viṣayaṅkaḷi yellām
koñcam uḷanūṟ payiṟciyuṭiyavarkaḷ eḷitiluṇarvārkaḷ. mēlum taṅkaḷukku immātiriyāṉa
turp pākkiyaṅkaḷi anupavikkumpaṭi nēriṭavum illi. ākiyiṉāl tāṉ taṅkaḷukku itu
putitākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
cūracēnaṉ:- namakkēṉ intamātiriyāṉa kaṣṭaṅkaḷellām varum? nāmtām eppōtum evaṉ
yuttattiṟku vantapōtilum caṉṉattamā yirukkiṟōmē! namakkeṉṉa?
cucīlaṉ:- taṅkaḷukkētāvatu varumeṉṟu collavilli. tāṅkaḷ itiyanupavittē yaṟivīrkaḷeṉṟu
tāṉ coṉṉēṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:-ām cucīlarē! nām aṉupavittatē illi.
cucīlaṉ:- nāṉ inta viṣayattic coṉṉatu etaṟkāka veṉṟāl nammuṭiya cēvakaṉ collukiṉṟaṉa
vellām vāstavamē\ eṉṟu kāṭṭum poruṭṭut tāṉ. mēlumivaṉ poy collukiṟavaṉ allaṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:- aṭē! cēvakā! pōṭā caritāṉ.
[cēvakaṉ pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
cūracēnaṉ:- cucīlarē! nammuṭiya eṇṇañcariyāy muṭintu viṭṭataṉṟō? iṉi nām pōkalāmā?
eṉṉa? nayavacakarē! muṉamāyirukkiṉṟīr!
nayavacakaṉ:- cakalamum cari. (taṉakkuḷ) makārājā avarkaḷ ceyyuṅ kāriyaṅkaḷukkuk
kēṭpāṉēṉ!
[yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
-----------------------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 1 / aintāṅkaḷam

iṭam:--nayavacanaṉ vīṭu
kālam:--piṟpakal
pāttiraṅkaḷ:-- nayavacanarum, cila kaṉavāṉkaḷum
mutaṟkaṉavāṉ:-- ēṉ! aiyā! nayavacanarē! nām itu variyilum intac cūracēṉaṉi nītimāṉā
yiruppāṉeṉṟu eṇṇi iruntataṟku nērvirōtamāka nammuṭiya pūrva rājā caṟkuṇa
vaḻutiyiyum, avar maṉivi cuntariyāriyum ciṟiccāliyil tāṉ aṭittirukkiṟāṉ eṉṟu
niṉittōm. iṉṟikku nāṉ vicāritta pōtu vēṟuvitamāka mantiri cucīlar vīṭṭu
vēlikkārarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷām; atikkēṭṭu eṉṉuṭiya vēlikkāraṉ iṉṟu eṉṉiṭañ
coṉṉāṉ. aḵtu eṉṉa nijamāyirukkumā?
iraṇṭāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- eṉṉa aiyā! nīr eṉṉavō collukiṟīr? nām nāḷikkē takunta ēṟpāṭukaḷ
ceytu inta makāpāviyi ūriviṭṭut turattiviṭṭu vaḻutiyārik kūppiṭuvōm.
nīrmāttiran tiriyamāyirum. oru ki pārppōm!
nayavacanaṉ:-- ām. aviyellā mirukkaṭṭum. nīr uṅkaḷuṭiya eṇṇatti niṟivēṟṟa
vēṇṭumeṉṟāl intamātiri kaṇṭapaṭi pēcuvatiṟ pirayōcaṉamilli. cūracēnaṉō, pōr vīrarōṭu
ippōtiṅkirukkiṉṟāṉ. antappōr vīrarkaḷi vēṟu eṅkēyāvatu aṉuppuvataṟkut takunta
yuktikaḷ ceytu avarkaḷ pōṉa piṟaku nīṅkaḷ kalakamō kilakamō ētōceytu rājāvi--tīrttu
viṭṭīrkaḷāṉāl appuṟam ellām cariyāyppōm. ( pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)

mūṉṟāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- pōm, aiyā! pōm. kokkiṉ taliyil veṇṇeyi vittu aḵtu uruki
appuṟam kokkup piṭikkiṟa tirukkaṭṭum! nīṅkaḷ māttiram pēcātiruṅkaḷ nāṉ tiriyamāy
rājā vītiyiṟ pavaṉivarum pōtu ivvīṭṭiyiṉāṟ kuttip--pōṭukiṟēṉ. appuṟam mēl
naṭakkiṟatip pārttukkoḷvōm. eṉṉi vēṇṭumeṉṟāṟ kolluvārakaḷ. nāṉ pōṉāṟ
pōkiṉṟēṉ! nāṭukāppiṉ poruṭṭu oruvaṉ maṭṭiṟ cātal nalamaṉṟō?
mutaṟkaṉavāṉ:-- ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nām avacarappaṭakkūṭātu. nayavacakar kūṟuvatiyum kēṭṭu
naṉṟāy ālōcaṉi paṇṇi appuṟam nammuṭiya eṇṇatti niṟivēṟṟikkoḷḷalām. pataṟāta kāriyam
citaṟātu!--āmām. pārttukkoḷḷum.
iraṇṭāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- avaruṭiya tātpariyam eṉṉaveṉṟāl, tāmacañ ceyvōmāṉāl oruvēḷi
aracaṉukkut terintu avaṉ mikavum cākkirati yāyiruntu viṭuvāṉākil nām niṉitta
eṇṇam niṟivēṟātu pōyviṭumē eṉpatutāṉ. vēṟoṉṟumilli.
mutaṟkaṉavāṉ:-- ivvaḷavum aracaṉukkut terintāl tāṉē kāriyam niṟivēṟātu pōkum?
iraṇṭāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- eṉṉavō? yār yāri nampalām? evaṉ eppaṭiyō? aracaṉuṭiya pirīti
campātikkiṟataṟkāka enta vañcakaṉāvatu inta yōcaṉiyippōy antap pātakaṉiṭam colli
vikkaṭṭum. ativiṭa vēṟu viṉi vēṇṭām. namakkuttāṉkēṭeṉṟu niṉikka vēṇṭām.
nayavacanarukkuṅ keṭutal; ūr muḻuvatuñ cuṭṭup pocukkic cāmpalākkip pōṭuvāṉ.
nayavacakaṉ:-- āmām. ataṟkāka ippōtu eṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭu meṉkiṟīr?
mūṉṟāṅkaṉavāṉ:-- atutāṉ nāṉ appōtē piṭittuc collukiṉṟēṉē! avaṉiccāykka
nāṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟēṉē!
nayavacanaṉ:-- ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nāṉoṉṟu collukiṉṟēṉ. kēḷuṅkaḷ. iṉṟikku intac
camācāratti, uṅkaḷuṭiya yōcaṉiyi, nammuṭiya iḷavaracaṉ cuntaranantaṉukku eṉṉuṭiya kumāraṉ
cantiramukaṉ mūlamākac colliyaṉuppuvōm. cakalattiṟkum avaṉ eṉṉa collukiṟāṉō atiyuṅ
kēṭṭu naṭappōm. itaṟku eṉṉa collukiṟīrkaḷ? uṅkaḷukku ellām ippaṭic ceyyac
cammatantāṉā?
yāvarum:-- paramacammatam. appaṭiyē ceyvōm.
nayavacanaṉ(eḻunatu) āṉāl eṉkumāraṉ cantiramukaṉiṭañ colli yaṉuppic cuntaranantaṉuṭiya
apippirāyattit terintu koṇṭu uṅkaḷukku yāṉ nāḷikkuc collukiṟēṉ. itu viṣayam
irakaciyamāyirukkaṭṭum veḷiyēṟiviṭap pōkiṟatu. pattiram! pattiram!!
yāvarum:-- antappaṭiyē mikavuñ cākkiratiyā yirukkiṟōm. atip paṟṟi nīṅkaḷ
yōcikkavē vēṇṭām.
[ yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar]
mutalaṅkam muṟṟiṟṟu
----------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 2 mutaṟkaḷam

iṭam:- muṟkūṟiya pūñcōli
kālam:- māli
pāttiram:- rūpāvati
rūpāvati:- (taṉakkuḷ) malar koyyappōṉa ampujāṭciyuṅ kanakamāliyum ivvaḷavu
nēramākiyum ēṉ varavilli?
(pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

eṉṟu kūṟiya taṅkaḷ vārttiyi maṟantu viṭṭīrkaḷō?-
(muṉam) avviṭattil oru mayil kalāpam virittu āṭukiṉṟatu. atip pōyp pārppēṉ;
ataṟkuḷ ivarkaḷum varaṭṭum.
[eḻuntu ciṟitutūram ulavukiṉṟāḷ.]
iḵteṉṉa putumi! ivviṭatti mititta uṭaṉē eṉṉuṭiya tēkam puḷakikkiṉṟatē! antō! itu
vaṉṟō cuntarānantaṉ nēṟṟu irunta iṭam? iṅkut tāṉē avaṉ mūrcci pōy viḻuntatu? -
eṉṉuṭiya takappaṉārum ittuṇik koṭiyarō? - avaṉuṭiya uruvam eṉ kaṇṇukku etirē
niṟkiṟatu pōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟatē! ō! cuntarānantaṉē! nēṟṟu uṉṉit taricittatu mutal
uṉṉip paricippatu ennāḷō ennāḷō eṉṟu ēṅki ēṅki varuntukiṉṟēṉ!
nēṟṟirāmuḻutum tūkkameṉpatu ciṟitum varavilli! eḻuntu nilāmuṟṟattil ulāviṉēṉ!
aiyō! koñcamēṉum karuṇi yeṉpatillāmaṟ cantiraṉ taṉatu nilavu ney peytu eṉatu
kāmākkiniyi vaḷarttut tuṉpappaṭuttiṉāṉ! eṉṉa ceyvēṉ? mīṭṭum paṭuttuk koḷvēṉ!
eḻuntiruppēṉ! ulāvuvēṉ! intappaṭi nēṟṟiravik kaḻittēṉ. iṉṟu eṉṉa ceyvēṉō? -
eṉṉuṭiya ācik kaṇṇāṉ cuntarānantaṉ oruvēḷi iṉṟikkum varuvāṉō? vantāl yāṉ avaṉi
iṉṉoru taramāvatu taricaṉam paṇṇi yāṟuvēṉ!
----------
* taṇṭiyalaṅkāravuri
(pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

----(muṉam)
eṉṉuṭiya tōḻimār oruvēḷi itik kēṭṭukkoṇṭu varuvārkaḷō? aiyō! eṉṉuṭiya intac
ceyalkaḷellām eṉ takappaṉārukkut terintu viṭṭāl eṉṉa ceyvēṉ! nāṉ keṭuvatē yaṉṟi
eṉṉuṭiya arumic cuntaranantaṉukkum kēṭu viḷiyumē! amma! ō! koṭumi! koṭumi!
---- atō ampujāṭci varukiṉṟāḷ. iṉimēl nāṉ itik kuṟittu pēcappaṭātu
----
[ ampujāṭci varukiṉṟāḷ.]
ampujāṭci! ēṉ ivvaḷavu tāmatam?
ampujāṭci:-- eṉṉavō? ammā! kanakamāliyum nāṉum iraṇṭu pērumāyp pōṉōm. avaḷi
yeṅkēyō kāṇēṉ. ituvarikkum varuvaḷ varuvaḷeṉṟu kāttuk koṇṭiruntēṉ. iṉṉum
varavilli. oruvēḷi eṉakku muntiyē iṅkē vantuviṭṭāḷō eṉṟu niṉittu vantēṉ. avaḷ
iṅkē vantāḷō?
[kanakamāli varukiṉṟāḷ]
rūpāvati:-- nī appaṭit tirumpippār yāreṉṟu!
ampujāṭci:-- eṉṉaṭi ammā! kākamāli! ituvarikkum eṅkē pōyiruntāy? uṉakkāka
evvaḷavu nāḻiki kāttukkoṇṭirukkiṟatu? rūpāvati taṉiyāyirukkiṟāḷē eṉṟu
vantuviṭṭēṉ.ētāvatu eṉ pērilē kōpamōṭi ammā?
kanakamāli:-- itaṟkuk kōpameṉṉa kōpam? nī eṉṉa periya tali pōkiṟa kāriyañ
ceytuviṭṭāy, ataṟku nāṉ uṉṉik kōpittukkoḷḷa? eṉṉavō ippaṭiyē pōṉēṉ. aṅkē
yōriṭattil iraṇṭu māṉkaḷ tuḷḷi viḷiyāṭiṉa. anta vēṭikkiyip pārttukkoṇṭē
iruntu viṭṭēṉ. atutāṉ koñcam tāmatamāyviṭṭatu. nammuṭiya rūpāvatiyi vekunāḻiki
kākkumpaṭi vittuviṭṭōmē eṉṟu varuttappaṭukiṟēṉ.
rūpāvati:-- inta aṟpa kāriyattiṟkāka nī varuttappaṭuvāṉēṉ? nāṉum iṅkē oru mayil
aḻakāyt tōki virittāṭiṟṟu. atip pārttukkoṇṭiruntēṉ.
ampujāṭci:-- ēṉ rūpāvati! nāṉ oṉṟu kēṭkiṟēṉ--kōpittuk koḷḷātiruntāṟ
collukiṟēṉ.
rūpāvati:-- kōpittuk koḷḷukiṟateṉṉa? collaṭi col.
ampujāṭci:- appaṭi yoṉṟumilli. vēṭikkiyāyttāṉ kēṭpō meṉṟu niṉittēṉ.
rūpāvati:- atittāṉ iṉṉateṉṟu collu, kēṭpōm. cīkkiram collu.
kanakamāli:- avaḷ eppoḻutum ippaṭittāṉ koñcam vīṇpiluk kellām paṇṇuvāḷ.
ampujāṭci:- appaṭi ettaṉi nāḷ ammā uṉṉiṭattilnāṉ pilukkup paṇṇi yirukkiṟēṉ?
rūpāvati:- veku! naṉṟāyirukkiṟatu aḵteṉṉi? nīyum ārampittu viṭṭāy! avaḷtāṉ
coṉṉāṟ collaṭṭumē! avaḷukku uṉṉic colla urimiyilliyā eṉṉa?
ampujāṭci:- coṉṉāṟ collukiṟāḷ. ataṟkoṉṟumilli. nāṉ collavanta teṉṉaveṉṟu
kēṭṭāl uṉ mukam eṉṉavō koñcam vāṭṭam aṭintatu pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatē; aḵtu etaṉālē
eṉṟu kēṭkattāṉ vāyeṭuttēṉ. ataṟkuḷḷē kanakamāli nāṉ pilukkup paṇṇuvatāka
niṉittuk koṇṭāḷ.
rūpāvati:- atutāṉ avaḷ viḷiyāṭṭāyc coṉṉāḷ; nīyēṉ ati maṟupaṭiyum maṟupaṭiyum
tūṇṭic collukiṟāy? eṉṉavō viṭṭu vēṟē yētāvatu pēcu.
kanakamāli:- ampujāṭci! nāṉ uṉṉit teriyātē colliviṭṭēṉ ammā! ivvaḷavu tūrañ
colluvāyeṉṟu terintiruntāl nāṉ pēciyirukkavē māṭṭēṉ.
rūpāvati:- cari, nīṅkaḷtām vātip pirativātikaḷām. nāṉ niyāyañ colvēṉām. eṅkē
iraṇṭu pērum uṅkaḷ vaḻakkic colluṅkaḷ. kēṭṭut tīrmāṉam colluvōm.
ampujāṭci:- eṉṉaṭiyammā! nī kēlipaṇṇukiṟāyē yoḻiya nāṉ kēṭṭati viṭṭuviṭṭāyē.
rūpāvati:_ nī appaṭi eṉṉa kēṭṭāy? eṉakkoṉṟum ñāpakam illiyē!
ampujāṭci:- ñāpakamilliyā? caritāṉ. nī eṅkaḷukkellām colluviyē ammā!
rūpāvati:- nī kēṭṭatē yiṉṉateṉṟu terintālaṉṟō nāṉ collak kūṭum ammā! eṉpēriṟ
kōpittuk koḷvatiṟ payaṉilli.
ampujāṭci:- āṉāṟ collukiṟēṉ. iṉṉorutaram; kavaṉamāykkēḷu. uṉ mukattil tiṉamum
irukkiṟa cantōṣakkaḷiyi iṉṟikku eṉṉavō kāṇōm! ataṉ kāraṇameṉṉa? collu.
rūpāvati:- ataṉ kāraṇamā? - (muṉam)
kanakamāli:- eṉṉa yōcikkiṟāy? collu.
ampujāṭci:- eṉṉa itu! aḻukiṟāyā? eṉṉa? ēṉ ammā! nāṅkaḷ iruvarum uṉ pakkattil
uṉakkut tuṇiyā yirukkiṟapōtu nī yaḻavēṇṭiya kāraṇameṉṉa? uṉṉi nāṅkaḷ yārum
eṉṉavāvatu coṉṉōmā? allāmaṟ pōṉāl nāṅkaḷ ivvaḷavu nāḻikiyā yuṉṉit taṉiyāy
viṭṭuviṭṭup pōy viṭṭō meṉṟu vicaṉappaṭukiṟāyā? atuvumallāviṭṭāl eviyāvatu
mirukaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu nī payantirukkalām eṉṟu colla etuvumilliyē. eṉatu arumit
taliviyē! rūpāvati! nī ivvāṟu varuttappaṭuvati nāṅkaḷ pārttuk koṇṭiruppōmō? uṉ
maṉattilirukkiṟa tuṉpatti nī yeṅkaḷiṭattiṟ collat takātō?
rūpāvati:- eṉakku atic colla nāveḻavilli. (muṉam)
[kaṇṇīr vaṭikkiṟāḷ.]
ampujāṭci:- aṭi! kanakamāli! nam rūpāvati meyyākavēyaḻukiṉṟāḷ. nī maṟṟit
tōḻimāriyum kūppiṭukiṟāyā? [iraṅkip pāṭukiṉṟāḷ.]

kanakamāli:- ampujāṭci! intac cōliyilē oru vaṉatēvati yuṇṭeṉṟu collak
kēṭṭirukkiṟōmē. eṅkē atutāṉ oru vēḷi yivaḷ taṉiyāyirukkum pōtu vantu
payamuṟuttiviṭṭup pōyiṟṟō? eṉṉavō?
ampujāṭci:- eṉakku appaṭi yirukkumeṉṟu tōṉṟavilli. appaṭiyāyiṉ ippaṭic
collamāṭṭāḷ. - ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, ēṉ rūpāvati! uḷḷa kāraṇatti eṅkaḷukku veḷiyiṭak
kūṭātā? nāṅkaḷ uṉ tōḻikaḷallamō?
[cuntarānantaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
rūpāvati:- (kaṇṇīrit tuṭittukkoṇṭu) aḵtu oṉṟum avvaḷavu vicēṣamilli. nām
pōkavēṇṭāmā? namakku nēramākavilliyā?
kanakamāli:-- tiṉamum varukiṟārkaḷē, anatap piḷḷikaḷ iṉṟikkum oruvēḷi vantālum
varuvārkaḷ. caṟṟē iruntu vēṇṭumeṉṟāṟ pārttuviṭṭup pōvōm. ētāvatu vicaṉappaṭṭup
pēcuvārkaḷ.kēṭpōm.
rūpāvati:-- avarkaḷip pārvi eṉṉa? kompu muḷittirukkiṟatō?
ampujāṭci:-- kanakamālitāṉ collukiṟāḷē. iruntutāṉ pārttuviṭṭuppōvamē. eṉṉavō?
eḷiyaṉ cuntarānantaṉ! avaṉi niṉikkiṟapōtellām eṉakku vicaṉamāyirukkiṟatu. avaṉ
taliyilē piramā ippaṭik kaṣṭappaṭumpaṭi eḻutiviṭṭāṉ!
rūpāvati:-- (vicaṉatti aṭakkikkoṇṭu) nēṟṟu aḻutu mūrccipōy viḻuntāṉē! avaṉā?
kanakamāli:-- āmām! avaṉtāṉ! aḻakāy iṉṉoruttaṉ maṭiyilē cāyntu koṇṭiruntavaṉ!
rūpāvati:-- ēṇṭi! kanakamāli! nī ippōtu coṉṉāyē intac cuntarānantaṉ, amapujāṭci
atti piḷḷi cukumāraṉ, ivarkaḷ iraṇṭu pērkaḷilum yār atika aḻakuṭāyavar?
kanakamāli:-- cuntarānantaṉ tāṉ. collukiṟataṟkuk kēṭpāṉēṉ?
[cukumāraṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
[avaṉum cuntarānantaṉ irukkumiṭam vantu oḷintu niṟkiṉṟāṉ.]
cuntarānantaṉ:--(maṟivil) koñcam pēcātiru!
rūpāvati:-- aracaṉ makaṉ eṉṟu collukiṟāyō?
kanakamāli:-- eṉakkeṉṉaṭi ammā! munti rājāpiḷḷiyā yiruntāl ippōteṉṉa ataṟku?
rūpāvati:-- (taṉakkuḷ) eṉṉē ulakiyal! araciḻantāri yavamatittal naṉṟāyiruntatu! celvañ
ciṟappō cemmi ciṟappō? (veḷiyāy) appuṟam, amapujāṭci caṇṭikku varappōkiṟāḷ!
cukumāraṉukkuc cuntarānantaṉuṭiya aḻakilli eṉṟu collukiṟāyē!
kanakamāli:-- ētammā! nīyē caṇṭiyiḻuttu viṭuvāypōrukkiṟatē!
ampujāṭci:-- eṉṉa! rūpāvati! ippaṭik kēli paṇṇukiṟāy!
rūpāvati:-- cari, iviyellā mirukkaṭṭum. kanakamāli! nāṉ oṉṟu kēṭkiṟēṉ.
colluviyā?
kanakamāli:-- eṉṉa? kēḷu! collakkūṭiyatāyiruntāl collukiṉṟēṉ.
rūpāvati:-- ampujāṭcitāṉ avaḷ attipiḷḷi cukumāraṉukku vāḻkkippaṭap pōkiṉṟāḷ.
nī yārukku vāḻkkippaṭap pōkiṉṟāy? cuntarānantaṉtāṉ aḻakāyirukkiṉṟāṉē1 nī ēṉ
avaṉik kalyāṇam ceytukoḷḷak kūṭātu?
ampujāṭci:- appaṭic collappōṉāl, rūpāvati! uṉakkum avaṉukkuntāṉ īṭu
cariyāyirukkiṟatu. uṉṉuṭiya aḻakukku ēṟṟavaṉtāṉ avaṉum! avaṉuṭiya aḻakukku
ēṟṟavaḷtāṉ nīyum!
rūpāvati:- (mukañcivantu) kanakamāliyum eṉṉa? aḻakiṟkuṟintavaḷō? avaḷum avaṉukku
ēṟṟavaḷtāṉ! oruvēḷi avaṉ ippōtu ciṟiccāliyi lirukkiṟāṉē eṉṟu eṇṇuvāḷō
eṉṉavō?
kanakamāli:- avi yellām vīṇ pēccu. rūpāvati! uṉakku iṣṭamiruntāl nī avaṉukku
vāḻkkippaṭu.
rūpāvati:- cari. ampujāṭci! kanakamālikūṭa vīṇ pēccu eṉṟu collukiṉṟāḷ!
nēramākiṟatu. vīṭṭiṟkup pōkavēṇṭum.
ampujāṭci:- rūpāvati! nī oru pāṭṭup pāṭu.
kanakamāli:- eṅkē rūpāvati! pāṭu.
rūpāvati:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

ampujāṭci:- pāṭṭu veku cukamā yirukkiṟatu.
kanakamāli:- iṉṟikku nām vantu veku nāḻikiyāy viṭṭatu. ākiyiṉālē nām cīkkiram
pōkavēṇṭum ammā!
rūpāvati:-cari. ēṉ ampujam! pōvamē!
ampujāṭci:- kuṟippeyarāyk kūppiṭṭāyāṉā luṉakkuk kuṟippiḷḷitāṉ piṟakkum!
pārttukkoḷ.
rūpāvati:- appaṭiyē piṟantālum oṉṟutāṉē yappaṭippiṟakkum. pōkaṭṭum. maṟṟip
piḷḷikaḷāvatu naṉṟāyp piṟakkumē yallavō? atu pōtum.
ampujāṭci:- ēṉ? tāmatam eṉṉa? pōkalāmē.
rūpāvati:- tāmatamoṉṟumilli. pōkalām. ampujāṭci nī iṉṟiravu māttiram eṅkaḷ
araṇmaṉiyil eṉṉōṭu taṅkiyiruntu viṭiyaṟkālam nīyuṉ vīṭṭiṟkup pōkalāmē.
ampujāṭci:- appaṭiyāṉāl nāṉippōtu vīṭṭiṟkup pōy eṉ takappaṉāriṭam anumati
peṟṟukkoṇṭu vantuviṭukiṟēṉ.
[rūpāvati, ampujāṭci, kanakamāli pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
cukumāraṉ:- (veḷivantu) nī vantu veku nāḻikiyāyviṭṭatō! ēṉ cuntarānantā! ippōtu
pēcikkoṇ ṭiruntārkaḷē, inta mūṉṟu peṇkaḷum iṉṉar eṉpatu uṉakkut teriyumō?
cuntarānantaṉ:- mantiri cucīlar peṇ orutti; avaḷtāṉ uṉ māmā peṇ. atu niccayamāyt
terikiṉṟatu. maṟṟiyiraṇṭu pērum yārō? naṉṟāyt teriyavilli.
cukumāraṉ:- eṉṉuṭiya māmā peṇ peyar ampujāṭci.- maṟṟa iraṇṭu pērkaḷilum atika
aḻakuḷḷavaḷā yiruntāḷē avaḷ tāṉ uṉṉi yikkōlamākkiya cūracēnaṉ putalvi. avaḷukku
avaḷuṭiya aḻakukkut takkapaṭi 'rūpāvati' yeṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. maṟṟavaḷ
'kanakamāli' yeṉṉum peyaruṭiyavaḷ. atit tavira vēṟoṉṟu mavaḷippaṟṟi eṉakkut
teriyātu.
cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉṉa! cantiramukaṉ iṉṟu ituvariyilum varavilli. ētāvatu avaṉ vīṭṭil
iṉṟikku vicēṣamuṇṭō? avaṉ orunāḷum ivvaḷavu tāmatam ceyyamāṭṭāṉē!
cukumāraṉ:- ataṟkeṉṉa ippōtu? avaṉ meḷḷattāṉ varaṭṭumē!
cuntarānantaṉ:- atippaṟṟi yoṉṟumilli. avaṉ cīkkiram varukiṟavaṉē eṉṟu coṉṉēṉ.
cukumāraṉ:- ō! cuntarānantā! nī yiṉṟu mālippoḻuti yevvāṟu kaḻittāy? ētēṉum
paṭittāyō?
cuntarānantaṉ:- paṭittilēṉ. āyiṉum em ālavāypperumāṉi vāyārap pāṭi tutittēṉ.
atuvē yeṉ maṉattiṟ ciṟitu amiti viḷittatu. atit tavirttu vēṟoṉṟuñ ceytilēṉ.
cukumāraṉ:- nī pāṭit tutittati nāṉ kēṭkalāmō? kēṭkalāmeṉiṉ atic col.
cuntarānantaṉ:-(pāṭukiṉṟāṉ)

cukumāraṉ:-ō! cuntarānantā! nī pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ mikavum naṉṟayirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi
yellām pūrvajaṉma puṇṇiyam!
cuntarānantaṉ:-(kaṇṇīr tatumpa) jaṉmāntarattil yāṉ puṇṇiyamuñ ceytēṉō? appā!
cukumārā! nī yaṟiyātu kūṟukiṉṟāy! yāṉ pūrvajaṉmattiṟ puṇṇiyañ ceytiruppēṉāyiṉ
ippōtu ēṉ intavitat tuṉpamellām anupavikkavēṇṭum? ivi yellām eṉṉuṭiya jaṉmāntara
pāvattiṉālēyā meṉpatu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
cukumāraṉ:- cari. inta eṇṇattiṟku, ippoḻutu iṭaṅ koṭuttōmō nēṟṟippōla ētēṉum
viparītam viḷikkum. appuṟam mikka tuṉpam varum. ākiyāl vēṟu viṣayattik kuṟittup
pēcuvōm.
cuntarānantaṉ:- iṉṟikku ētāvatu vicēṣam irukkattāṉ vēṇṭum. illāviṭiṟ cantiramukaṉ
ivvaḷavu tāmatam ceyya māṭṭāṉ.
cukumāraṉ:- avaṉum varukiṟa camayantāṉē! vantuviṭuvāṉ. itu kiṭakkaṭṭum. atu mūṉṟu
peṇkaḷum pēcik koṇṭatiliruntu oṉṟu paṭukiṉṟatu.
cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteṉṉi? eṉakkoṉṟum tōṉṟavilliyē! eṅkē? aḵtu iṉṉateṉṟu collu.
kēṭpōm.
cukumāraṉ:- atuvā? collukiṉṟēṉ kēḷ. aracaṉ puttiri rūpāvatikku uṉ pēriṟ
koñcam piriyamirukkiṟatu eṉpatu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu.
cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteppaṭik kaṇṭāy? veku naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatē!
cukumāraṉ:- avarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭirunta campāṣaṇiyiliruntu nāṉ anumāṉittuk koṇṭēṉ.
cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭiyāṉāl eṉakkēṉ tōṉṟavilli?
cukumāraṉ:- aḵteṉṉiyō? yāṉaṟiyēṉ. eṉ maṉattil tōṉṟiṉatic coṉṉēṉ. atittavira
vēṟilli.
cuntarānantaṉ:- āyiṉ amiyum. ovvoruvarun tamatu maṉattil tamakku vēṇṭiyaṉavaṟṟi
yellām niṉittuk koḷḷalām. – āyiṉum eṉakkut tōṉṟukiṟa maṭṭil anta mūṉṟu
peṇkaḷum nām nēṟṟip pēcik koṇṭirunta caṅkatikaḷi yoḷittuk kēṭṭiruppārkaḷō
veṉpatu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl eṉṉikkuṟitta rūpāvati 'nēṟṟu mūrcci pōy
viḻuntāṉē, avaṉā?' eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ.
cukumāraṉ:- āṉāl veku nallatāyiṟṟē!
cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteppaṭi yappā?
cukumāraṉ:- uṉ pēril irakkaṅkoṇṭu oru vēḷi taṉ takappaṉārik kēṭṭu uṉṉic
ciṟiccāliyi ṉiṉṟum viṭṭuviṭac collak kūṭum. ataṉālētāṉ nallateṉṟēṉ.
cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭittāṉ avaḷ ceyvāleṉpateṉṉi? oru vēḷi yeṉṉuṭaya caṅkatikaḷi
yellān taṉ takappaṉāriṭañ colli yeṉṉi yiṉṉum atikamāṉa tuṉpaṅkaḷukku ēṉ
uṭpaṭutta māṭṭāḷ? appaṭic ceytālum ceyyak kūṭumē?
cukumāraṉ:- nāṉ avaḷippaṟṟik kēḷvippaṭṭa maṭṭil avaḷ nallavaḷē eṉpatu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
ākiyāl nī niṉikkiṟapaṭi avaḷ ceyyamāṭṭāḷ eṉpataṟku ākṣēpamē yilli.
cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭiyāṉāl eṉ ālavāyt tēvaṉi yāṉ pāṭit tutittatu
palittuviṭṭateṉṟu collalām. appā! atō! aṅkē varukiṉṟāṉ cantiramukaṉ.
[cantiramukaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ.]
cukumāraṉ:- eṅkē?
cantiramukaṉ:- vantaṉam cuntarānantā!
cukumāraṉ:- ēṉ, cantiramukā! uṉṉi evvaḷavu kālam etirpārttuk koṇṭiruppō meṉṟu
niṉittāy?
cantiramukaṉ:- nāṉ uṉ vīṭṭiṟpōy uṉṉit tēṭiṉēṉ. avviṭattilē nī yillāmiyāl iṅkē
tāṉ vantiruppā yeṉṟu niṉittu vantēṉ. atutāṉ nāḻikiyāyviṭṭatu. akkuṟṟattip
poṟuppīrkaḷeṉṟu niṉikkiṉṟēṉ. mēlum nāṉ tāmatittu vantataṟku vēṟu kāraṇamum uṇṭu.
aḵteṉṉeṉṟāl: eṉṉuṭiya tantiyār eṉṉi yaḻittu irakaciya camācāra moṉṟu colli,
atippaṟṟi uṉṉiṭattil terivikkumpaṭi āṇiyiṭṭaṉuppiṉār.
cuntarānantaṉ:- aḵteṉṉa appaṭi yirakaciyamāṉa camācāram?
cukumāraṉ:- ō! cuntarānantā! nī yeṇṇiyapaṭi, cantiramukaṉum ētō vicēṣa camācāram
koṇṭuvan tirukkiṉṟāṉē!
cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭi yallāviṭṭāl avaṉ kālakaraṇam ceyyak kāraṇamilliyē! cari
cantiramukā! camācāra miṉṉateṉṟu col. kēṭpōm.
cantiramukaṉ:- iṉṟu eṉṉuṭiya vīṭṭil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kaṉavāṉkaḷ vantu eṉṉuṭiya
tantiyāroṭu cilavaṟṟik kuṟittup pēciṉārkaḷ. avi yeṉṉaveṉṟāl, uṉṉi iccūracēnaṉ
ciṟiyilaṭittiruppatum, uṉ peṟṟōri valliyam palatirivaṉattut turattiyatum, niyāyamalla
veṉpatum ivvita aniyāyatti niṟuttuvataṟkut takka yuktikaḷ yāviyō avaṟṟiṉ nāṭi
muyaṟci ceyyavēṇṭu meṉpatuvēyām. itu viṣayattil uṉ yōcaṉi yeṉṉaveṉṟu eṉ tantiyār
kēṭṭuvarumpaṭi eṉṉiṭañ coṉṉār.
cuntarānantaṉ:- ak kaṉavāṉkaḷ eṇṇiya yōcaṉikaḷellām nallaṉavēyām. āyiṉum cūracēnaṉ
eṉṉiyum eṉ peṟṟōriyum kolikkaḷam paṭātu viṭṭiruttalē pērutaviyām. ākavē
yittaṉmiyāṉa upakārikku virōtamāka ētēṉuñ ceyya muyalutal ceynnaṉṟi kōṟalām.

eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruttalip palamuṟiyum namatu āciriyar vittiyācākarap pulavar
eṭutturit tirukkiṉṟaṉarē! aḵtuṇarntiruntum nī yivvāṟu kūṟutal nītiyākumō?
------------
* tirukkuṟaḷ
cukumāraṉ:- appā! cuntarānantā! nī kūṟuvatu mikavum naṉṟu! naṉṟu! inta makāvañcakaṉ
cūracēnaṉ viṣayattil nītiyeṉpatē pārtta loṇṇātu. tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār kūṟuvaṉavellām
nalloḻukka muṭiyavarkaṭkēyām. tīyoḻukka muṭiyavarkaṭku nītiyētu? niyāyamētu?
tarumamētu?
cuntarānantaṉ:- ō, cukumārā! nāṉ colliyati maṟantuviṭṭu nī yivvāṟu kūṟalāmō?
cūracēnaṉ tarumacinti yuṭiyavaṉ eṉpataṟku ākṣēpamumuṇṭō? eṉṉik kollātu
viṭṭiruppatē avaṉuṭiya nalla maṉattiṟkut takkacāṉṟu pakarum.
cukumāraṉ:- kolluvatu pāvam, ākavē kollāmi pāvamilliyē
yaṉṟi atu puṇṇiyamākumō? iṅṅaṉaṅ kollā tiruttalōṭu oruvaṉ etik
kollātirukkiṉṟāṉō ataṟku utaviyum naṉmiyuñ ceyvāṉāṉāl atu puṇṇiyam. maṟṟippaṭi
kollā tiruttal orunāḷum puṇṇiya mākātu.
cuntarānantaṉ:- ō, cukumārā! iṉṉuṅ kēṭpāy! eṉṉik kollā tiruttalēyaṉṟi yeṉṉic
ciṟiccāliyi ṉiṉṟum veḷiyē pōyulāvutaṟkum eṉatu arumi naṇparkaḷākiya uṅkaḷuṭaṉ
pēcik kaḷippataṟkum uttaravu koṭuttiruttal uṉakkut teriyātō?
cantiramukaṉ:- (taṉakkuḷ) naṭuvil nāmoṉṟuñ collakkūṭātu.
cukumāraṉ:- iṉṉoruviṣaya muṉakkut teriyātē! intak koliñaṉ uṉṉic ciṟiccāliyilē
yiṭavēṇṭumeṉṟum, veḷiyēpōka viṭakkūṭāteṉṟum, collittāṉ mutalmutalil uttaravu
ceytāṉām. piṟaku eṉṉuṭiya māmaṉār cucīlar aracaṉi nōkki nīr appaṭic ceyvīrāṉāl
jaṉaṅkaḷ ummik koṭiyavareṉṟu niṉikkakkūṭum eṉṟu coṉṉatik kēṭṭapiṟpāṭu tāṉ ippaṭik
kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭāṉām.
cuntarānantaṉ:- irukkaṭṭum. ellāvaṟṟiṟkum cantiramukaṉi muṟṟiluṅ kēṭpōm. ēṉ
cantiramukā! aracaṉ koṭumiyi niṟuttuvataṟku ak kaṉavāṉkaḷ ētēṉum yukti
ceytirukkkiṉṟārkaḷāmē?
cantiramukaṉ:- ām. appaṭittāṉ coṉṉār. aḵteṉṉeṉṟāl: oru kaṉavāṉ aracaṉ vītiyil
ulāvarumpōtu avaṉik kuttip pōṭuvatākac coṉṉārām. maṟṟik kaṉavāṉkaḷum, atu
cariyeṉṟu anumōtittārkaḷām. āṉāl, eṉ tantiyār māttiram pataṟakkūṭāteṉṟum,
itikkuṟittu iṉṉum yōcaṉi ceyyavēṇṭu meṉṟum coṉṉārām. iti yoruvar
ottukkoṇṭārām. maṟṟi yiruvarum ottukkoḷḷa villiyām. ataṉpēril itikkuṟittu
uṉṉiṭattil teriyap
paṭutti, itaṟku nī yeṉṉa collukiṉṟāyō ataṉpaṭi naṭappatāka ellōrum tīrmāṉittārkaḷām.
avvaḷavutāṉ. nī yeṉṉa collac collukiṉṟāyō ati yeṉ tantiyāriṭañ collukiṉṟēṉ.
cuntarānantaṉ:- ētappā! mika vintiyā yirukkiṉṟatē! oṉṟum payaṉpaṭum yuktiyāyk
kāṇappaṭa villiyē! itik kuṟittuc colluvataṟku eṉak koṉṟun tōṉṟavilli. ākiyāl uṉ
tantiyār nayavacanariṭam eṉ vantaṉaṅkaḷic colli avar iṣṭam eppaṭiyō appaṭiyē naṭakkumpaṭi
coṉṉēṉ eṉṟu collu, cantiramukā!
cukumāraṉ:- cari. appuṟam cantiramukā! uṉakkut teriyātēyoru caṅkati! nēṟṟuc
cuntarānantaṉ mūrccipōy viḻuntatu mutal nām pēcikkoṇṭa camācāramellā vaṟṟiyum
aracaṉ puttiri rūpāvati, eṉ māmā peṇ ampujāṭci, kanakamāli yinta mūṉṟu pērum
oṟṟuk kēṭṭuk koṇṭiruntirukkiṟārkaḷ. nām atip pārkkavilli. iṉṟikku nāṉum
cuntarānantaṉum avarkaḷ pēcik koṇṭati yoḷittiruntu kēṭṭōm. atutāṉ eṅkaḷukkut
teriyum.
cantiramukaṉ:- appaṭiyā! ateppaṭik kēṭpārkaḷappā! āccariyamā yirukkiṉṟatē!
cukumāraṉ:- ateṉṉavō! teriyātappā! vēṭikkitāṉ!
cuntarānantaṉ:- ēṉ cantiramukā! avarkaḷ kēṭṭataṉālē ētāvatu keṭutal varumō? nāṉ
aracaṉik koñcam varuttattiṉāl vitēṉē!
cukumāraṉ:- nāṉtāṉ coṉṉēṉē! nallatē yoḻiyakkeṭutalilli. rūpāvatitāṉ atika
irakkamuḷḷavaḷē!
cantiramukaṉ;- āmām! eṉakkum appaṭittāṉ tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. avaḷ aḻakukkelliyum
aṉpukkākaramumāvaḷē!
cuntarānantaṉ:- appaṭiyāyiṉ nallatutāṉ. nīṅkaḷ collukiṟatum, nāṉ collukiṟatu
mellām iṉimēl teriyum.
cukumāraṉ:- itu nūṟṟilorupēccu! pārppamē!
[yāvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 2 / iraṇṭāṅkaḷam

iṭam:- irācaputtiriyiṉ kaṉṉimāṭam
kālam:- naḷḷiravu
pāttiram:- rūpāvati
[ampujāṭci yorupuṟam tuyilpurikiṉṟāḷ.]
rūpāvati:- (taṉakkuḷ) aḻakum iṉpamum aṉpum niṟintacuntarānantaṉē!
cuntarānantaṉē! ikkuṇaṅkaḷ uṉṉiṭattil iruppati aṟintu tāṉō uṉṉi yuṉpeṟṟōrkaḷ
'cuntarānantaṉ' eṉṟu aḻittārkaḷ? aiyō! uṉṉuṭiya malarntavataṉattiyum matura vacaṉattiyuṅ
kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum āṉantappaṭuvatu eṉṟikkō eṉṟikkō eṉṟu nāṉ uṉṉi niṉittu ēṅkuvati
nīyaṟiyāyō? eṉ takappaṉār uṉṉi ciṟiyiliṭṭiruppataṉāl varuntukiṉṟāyō? eṉ takappaṉār
koṭiyavarāyiruttal paṟṟi nāṉuṅ koṭiyavaḷā yiruppēṉeṉṟu eṇṇuvāyō? antō!
aracamarattip piṭitta caṉiyaṉ ataṉkaṇ amarnta piḷḷiyāriyum piṭittatāmeṉṟu ulakōr
kūṟum vacaṉampōla avar koṭumi eṉṉiṭattum irukkumeṉṟu cantēkippāyō? uṉṉi
mutalmutaliṟ kaṇṭatutoṭṭu eṉmaṉam eṉṉiṭattilēyē yirāmal uṉ mukāravintattiyē
pārttut taṉṉiyum eṉṉiyum maṟantu mayaṅki niṟkiṉṟatu kaṇṭiliyō? accō! cuntaraṉē!
āṉantaṉē! cuntarānantaṉē! uṉ mukamatiyiṉ amirtatti yuṇṭu uyir tarippa tennāḷō?---
(muṉam)
ō! cakōraṅkaḷē! nīvirellām cantira kiraṇaṅkaḷi kaḷippuṭaṉuṇṭu cerukkit
tirikiṉṟīrkaḷē! umakkuḷ eṉṉippōl āṟṟātu ēṅkiyiraṅkuvā rilliyō? ammavō! maṉmataṉ
kuyiṟkāḷa mūtukiṉṟāṉē! iṉi yeṅṅaṉamōṭi yoḷippēṉ? intappāvi maṉmataṉ
uruviliyāyirukkum pōtē ariviyarkaḷi ippāṭu paṭuttukiṉṟāṉē! uruvōṭu
kūṭiyiruppāṉāyiṉ eṉpōliyar piḻittaluṅ kūṭumō? appavō! iṉṟu māli eṉṉuyir
niliyākiya cuntarānanta mūrttiyit taricikkum eṇṇattōṭu pōyiṉēṉē! appaṭip pōyum
eṉatarumit talivaṉi maṟupaṭiyuṅ kāṇṭaṟkillātu pōyiṟṟē! pōyiṟṟē!! iṉiyeṉṉa
ceyvēṉ? evvitamāṟuvēṉ? nāṉ pūṅkāvaṉattiliruntapōtu eṉṉuṭiya tōḻiyarēṉum iṉṉuñ
ciṟitu atika nēraṅ kaḻittu vantārilliyē!-(muṉam) ō!mātavi maramē! nī ceyta puṇṇiyam
nāṉ ceytilaṉē! nīyuṉ ṉaṭiyil eṉ piriyanāyakaṉi vittiruntaṉiyē! nāṉ avaṉi yippōtu
kāṇap peṟuvēṉē avaṉi eṉtaliyil vittuk koṇṭaṉṟō kaḷikkūttāṭuvēṉ!-(muṉam) ō!
kaṇkāḷ! nīvirēṉumoru muṟi yeṉatu talivaṉit taricittīr! cevikāḷ! nīvir orutaram avaṉ
pēcak kēṭṭīr! ātaliṉ nīvir pākkiyavāṉkaḷē yāvīr! uṅkaḷ pākkiyam eṉatu maṟṟip
poṟikaḷukkuṅ kiṭikka villiyē! avi nummāṭṭup pōṟāmiyāṟ puḻuṅki
yēṅkukiṉṟaṉavē! yāṉē yivvāṟu varuntā niṟka, avaṟṟi āṟṟuvār yāvar? aiyavō!
karuṇiyillākkāmaṉum iruḷ yāṉi muṉṉēlit teṉṟalun tērēṟip pōrkkaḷattup pukkaṉaṉē!

ampujāṭci:- (kaṇ viḻittukkoṇṭu) eṉṉa? ammā! rūpāvati! nīyaṅkē nilā muṟṟattil
eṉṉa ceytu koṇṭirukkiṉṟāy?
rūpāvati:- ampujāṭci! nī yeṉṉa paṭuttukkoṇṭavuṭaṉē yuṟaṅki viṭṭaṉiyē! eṉakku
ituvarikkun tūkkamē varavilli. nāṉ tūkkam varukiṟataṟkāka evvaḷavō muyaṉṟu pārttum
varavilli.
aḵtirukkaṭṭum.
(ampujāṭci yeḻukiṉṟāḷ.)
ampujāṭci! atō pārttāyā? viki yāṟṟiṉ kaṇ veḷḷam pōkiṉṟatu!
ampujāṭci:- āmām. nām poḻutuviṭintatum putuppuṉalāṭak kaṉaka māliyiyuṅ
kūṭṭikkoṇṭu maṟṟit tōḻimārōṭum pōvōm.
rūpāvati:- putuppuṉalāṭuta lirukkaṭṭum. maṟṟippaṭi nīyanta natiyip pār! cantira
kiraṇaṅkaḷ taṇṇīriṟ paṭuvataṉālē atu paḷa paḷaveṉṟu miṉṉukiṉṟatu! eṅkē! ampujāṭci nī
vittiyācākarap pulavar ceytaṉaveṉṟu cila yāṟṟuvarip pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭuvāyē!
avaṟṟiloṉṟupāṭu. kēṭpōm.
ampujāṭci:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

rūpāvati! nīyum ētāvatu vēṟu varippāṭṭuppāṭu.
rūpāvati:- (pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

antō! paṭukkiyaṟiyiviṭṭuvantu vekunēramāy viṭṭatē! ampujāṭci! cevilittāy vantu
nampaṭukki vēṟuvitamāyiruppatip pārttāl eṉṉa colvāḷō? iṉiyivviṭattilirukkap
paṭātu.
[iruvarum pōkiṉṟaṉar.]
-------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 2 / mūṉṟāṅkaḷam

iṭam:- ciṟiccāli
kālam:- naḷḷiravu
pāttiram:- cuntarānantaṉ
cuntarānantaṉ:- eṉṉuṭiya tantiyun tāyum innēram eṅkirukkiṟārkaḷō?
ippōtiravāyiṟṟē! iravil tīyavilaṅkukaḷ vaṉattilulāvumē! avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum avarkaḷ evvāṟu
tappiṉarō? avarkaḷ uyirōṭiruntālaṉṟō cakalamum iṉimiyāy muṭiyum? illiyāyiṉ
iṉpameṉpatētu? vāḻkkiyeṉpatētu?
(muṉam) iṉṟu nayavacanar tamatu puttiraṉ cantiramukaṉ mūlamāyccolli yaṉuppiya viṣayam
mikappayaṅkaramā yirukkiṉṟatē! nayavacakar verunāḷi yanupavamuḷḷavarātaliṉ avviṣayattil
avaratu iṣṭappaṭiyē avarceyyaṭṭum! yāṉ intak kāriyaṅkaḷilellān taliyiṭutal
matiyīṉamēyām. – ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nāḷip pūñcōlikkaṇ- 'pūñcōli' yeṉṟavuṭaṉ eṉmaṉam
pūrikkiṉṟatē! uṭalcilirkkiṉṟatē! ā! rūpāvati! rūpāvati! uṉṉip pārttatumutal
yāṉ, putumiyāṉatōr maṉōvikāratti yaṭintēṉ! atiyeṉṉeṉṟu eṭutturippēṉ? nī
aḻakiṉukku elliyāyuḷḷaṉi yeṉpatupaṟṟi 'rūpāvati' eṉṟu niṉakkup
peyariṭṭārkaḷpōlum! ō! nalleḻil peṟṟa naṅki nāyakamē! aṟivu niṟi orppuk
kaṭippiṭiyeṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭum āṭavar kuṇam nāṉkum puṉalōṭuvaḻip
puṟcāyntāṟpōla niṉṉikkaṇṭavuṭaṉ! cāyntuviṭṭaṉa! niṉṉik kāṇṭalum kāmuṟṟēṉ! atu
mutal yāṉ niṉṉiyiṉṟiyamiyātava ṉāyiṉēṉ! aṅṅaṉam uṉṉiyiṉṟiyamiyātu
niṉṟavēṭkiyāṉatu eṉatu ellā uṇarviṉiyum nīkkit tāṉēyāy nāṇvaḻikkācupōlavum, nīr
vaḻi mitavipōlavum eṉṉi yiḻāniṉṟatu! nīyum eṉmītu kātal koṇṭirukkiṉṟā yeṉṟu
nampuvataṟkuc ciṟitu iṭamuḷatu. āyiṉum ataṉi yāṉ evvāṟu niccayamāy nampuvaṉō? nī
yuṉ pāṅkimāruṭaṉ pēcikkoṇṭatiliruntum uṉakku eṉmītiṟ ciṟitu iyaṟki yaṉpuḷateṉṟu
kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ. atupaṟṟi yāṉ uṉṉiṭattil evvāṟu kātal kūṟuvēṉ? aiyō! yāṉ uṉṉi
yeṉṉuyirt tuṇiyāyp peṟa niṉippatu muṭavaṉ komputtēṉi yiccittu ataṉi yavaṉpeṟa
niṉippatu pōlum! uṉ tantiyō eṉṉi eppoḻutu kolikkaḷam paṭukkalā meṉṟu yōciyā
niṟkiṉṟāṉ! yāṉō uṉṉik kippiṭittu eṉatu taliviyāyk koḷvatu eppaṭiyō eṉṟu
yōciyā niṟkiṉṟēṉ! yāṉ uṉṉiyeṉatu pirāṇa nāyakiyāyp peṟavēṇṭu meṉṟāṟ
cuntaramūrttināyaṉār paravi nācciyārip peṟṟatu pōlat tāṉ peṟa vēṇṭum! vēṟu
vakiyilli. teyvantā ṉutaviceyya vēṇṭum ivviṣayattil.
(muṉam)
[pāṭukiṉṟāṉ]

ō! īṇṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ yāvum nīyāyp pariṇamittut tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉavē! itō niṉ koṇṭalit
taṇṭaliyi veṉṟa pūṅkūntal tōṉṟukiṉṟatu! atōniṉ kūṉpiṟinutalum, kuḻiḻi nikar
tuṇṭamum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa! āṇṭu avaḷ kūrmi taruvēli vāḷi vaṇṭi makaratti nikar
miyeḻutu viḻikaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉavē!
(pāṭukiṉṟāḷ)

ivi yellām poyttōṟṟaṅkaḷāmē! eṉceyvēṉ? uṭal vetumpukiṉṟatē! aiyō! caṟṟu
veḷiyēṟit taṇmatiyiṉ kuḷircciyiyum, naṟunteṉṟaṟ kuḷircciyiyum
anupavikkalāmeṉṟāṟ katavaṭittut tāḻiṭṭirukkiṉṟatē! eṅṅaṉam eṉatu vemmi
yāṟuvēṉ?-(muṉam)
[pāṭukiṉṟāṉ.]

yāṉ ivvāṟē yēṅkip pulampikkoṇṭiruppēṉāyiṉ kāvalāḷar viḻittukkoṇṭu eṉṉi
vivārkaḷē! eṉ ceyvēṉ? ituvumeṉ talivitiyō! teyvamē! itaṟku muṉ yāṉ
ciṟiccāli-yiliruppati oru poruṭṭā yeṇṇilēṉ; eṉṉip peṟṟōratu piriviyē
niṉittu vāṭiṉēṉ. ippoḻutō, ciṟiccāli ciṟiccāliyēyāyviṭṭatu! peṟṟōr
pirivēyaṉṟi aracaṉ makaḷ rūpāvatiyiṉ āciyum eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟatuvē!-(muṉam) ō! ētō
captam kēṭkiṉṟatē! kāvalāḷar tuyiloḻintārkaḷō? iṉimēl iṅkē yiruppatu takutiyaṉṟu.
[cuntarānantaṉ pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
iraṇṭām aṅkam muṟṟiṟṟu.
--------------------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 3 mutaṟkaḷam

iṭam:- pāṇṭiyaṉaraṇmaṉi
kālam:- kāli
pāttiraṅkaḷ:- cūracēnaṉum,cucīlaṉum
cūracēnaṉ:- cucīlarē! nām aṉṟu pēcikkoṇṭataṟku nērvirōtamāka iṉṟikku oṉṟu
kēḷvippaṭṭōm. atu mutal eṉṉavō ematu maṉam varuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.
cucīlaṉ:- appaṭi makārājā avarkaḷ varuttappaṭumpaṭiyāṉa viṣayametuvum campavittatākat
tōṉṟavilliyē!
cūracēnaṉ:- nām kāṭṭil turattiya avviruvarum puli mutaliya mirukaṅ kaṭku iriyāy
viṭuvarē yallāmaṟ piḻikka māṭṭāreṉṟaṉṟō niṉittōm? appaṭiyirukka, iṉṟu nammuṭiya
oṟṟar vantu avarkaḷ iruvarum cōḻanāṭṭi liruppatākac collukiṉṟārkaḷ! avarkaḷ eppaṭip
pōyirukkak kūṭumō? teriyavilli. inta viṣayam kātiṟ paṭṭatu mutal emakkuk kavaṟci
peritāyiṟṟu. cucīlarē! itaṟku eṉṉa ceyyalām? naṉṟāy ārāyntu collumiṉ!
cucīlaṉ:- makārājā avarkaḷ ivvaṟpa viṣayaṅkaḷuk kellām kavaṟci yaṭivāṉēṉ? tāṅkaḷ
yārukku añcavēṇṭum? etaṟkup payappaṭavēṇṭum? caṟkuṇavaḻutiyum cuntariyārum
piḻittut tām irukkiṉṟārkaḷō eṉapatē mutal mutaliṟ cantēkam. appaṭiyē yavarkaḷ
piḻittiruntāl tāṉ eṉṉa? aṉṟiyavarkaḷ cōḻanāṭṭil vīrēntira cōḻa ṉutavipeṟṟāltāṉ
eṉṉa? namakku payamā? nām eppōḻutum payappaṭa vēṇṭuvatē yilli.
cēvakaṉ:- (araṇmaṉi vāyililiruntu) ōy! nīr yār?
pulavar:- nāmtāṉ makāvittuvāṉ vittiyācākarap pulavar. aracaṉmītu kavipāṭip pōntuṉam.
itaṉi yiṟivaṉiṭan teritti.
cēvakaṉ:- ē! pulavar! rācāvukku teriyappaṭuttiṉā namakku eṉṉā koṭuppiya?
pulavar:- emakku maṉṉavar perumāṉ makiḻciṟantu aḷiyā niṟkum paricil
nāṉkiloṉṟīkutum. peṟuti!
cēvakaṉ:- ē! pulavar! ateṉṉa? peṟuti! pakuti! vikuti! atellām iṅkē nammiṭattilē
ilakkaṇam paṭikkātēyum! collum nērē! evvaḷavu koṭuppīr?
pulavar:- atutāṉ nāliloṉṟu taruvōm eṉṟu appoḻutē coṉṉōmē! teriyavilliyā?
cēvakaṉ:-cari. pulavar! mōcam ceyyakkūṭātu. pārttukkoḷḷum. pattiram!
[cēvakaṉ uḷḷē pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
pulavar:- caritāṉ, pō. (taṉakkuḷ) entap periya maṉuṣaṉ vīṭṭilum
inta mātiri ciṟcila cēvaka nāykaḷiruntu koṇṭu empōliyarik kāṇṭaluṅ kurittu
veḷḷeṉṟu mēlēviḻat talip paṭukiṉṟaṉa!
(pāṭukiṉṟār)

ō! ivvaracaṉ eṉṉavō! makā kāval vittirukkiṉṟāṉ! muṉṉirunta vaḷḷal caṟkuṇavaḻuti yām
eppoḻutum tārāḷamāy araṇmaṉikkuṭ pōkalām eṉṟu uttaravu ceytiruntāṉ!--ivaṉukku
nammi yiṉṉun teriyātē! terintāl ivaṉum avvāṟu uttaravu ceyyakkūṭum.
cēvakaṉ:- makārācā avakaḷukku āyiraṅkōṭi vantaṉam.
cūracēnaṉ:- aṭā! cēvakā! eṉṉa caṅkati? cīkkiramāyc collaṭā.
cēvakaṉ:- makārācā avakaḷiṭattil yārō makā vittuvāṉām, vittiyācākarap pulavarām.
avar vantirukkiṟār. itu viṣayam camūkattiṟkut teriyappaṭuttiṉēṉ.
cucīlaṉ:- ē! cēvakā! nīpōy avari uḷḷē varaccol.
[cēvakaṉ veḷiyē pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
tāṅkaḷ itikkuṟittu oṉṟum ippoḻutu yōcaṉi ceyya vēṇṭuvatē yilli. appuṟam
vēṇṭumeṉṟāṟ pēcik koḷḷalām.
cēvakaṉ:- ē! pulavar! pōm! varaccolli makārācā uttaravu ceytiṭṭār. coṉṉata
maṟantiṭātēyum! pattiram!
[pulavar uḷḷē pōkiṉṟār.]
pulavar:- cukapōka cukuṇa maṉmatarūpa makārāja cūracēna varmar camūkattiṟku aṭiyēm kakaṉa
mūtaṇṭavētaṇṭa piramāṇṭa meṅkaṇum kaṉapukaḻ paṭitta vuravēm imayamuta līḻamvari yivarkku
nikarevarumili yeṉṟu colappeṟṟa takiyēm kavimaṟakkuñcaraṅ kalvik kaḷañciyam ātalpaṟṟi
vittiyācākarap pulavareṉu
mēlāṉa vayiṟāṉa muṭiyarāvēm vantaṉan tantaṉam tuntaṉam peṟṟēkavē!
cūracēnaṉ::- ē! pulavarē! eṉṉa maṭamaṭaveṉṟu umpāṭṭiṟku aṭittuk koṇṭu pōkiṟīrē!
pulavar:- eṉṉa! makārājā avarkaḷ uttaravu teriyavilliyē! ematu pāṭṭiṟkāka
emmiyaṭikkiṟatā? teriyātu vantuviṭṭēṉ! makārājāvē! poṟuttaruḷa vēṇṭum!
poṟuttaruḷavēṇṭum!
(naṭuṅkukiṉṟār)
cucīlaṉ:- ōy! kattātēyum! cummā irum!- ivar nallavittuvāṉ! camūkattilē pēciyatu
teriyavilli. ataṉālētāṉ ippaṭip pēciṉār! vēṟoṉṟu milli.
cūracēnaṉ:- eṅkē nām pāṇṭiyaṉōṭu ceyta yuttattil aṭinta veṟṟiyip paṟṟip pāṭiya
kaviyic collac collum cucīlarē! kēṭpōm.
cucīlaṉ:- ē! pulavarē! pataṟāmal, payappaṭāmal, tiriyamāy vēkamāyc collāmal, niṟuttic
collum, nī makārājā avarkaḷ pēriṟ ceyta kaviyi.
pulavar:- koñcam kaṇ pārttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum!
cucīlaṉ:- aviyellām yōciyātēyum! kanakāpiṣēkam ceyyac collukiṉṟēṉ. kaviyic
collum.
pulavar:- paḷa paḷā! mikaviyantaṉam!
cucīlaṉ:- itutāṉē emakkuk kōpam varukiṉṟatu! collumeṉṟāl uṭaṉē colla vēṇṭāmā?
pulavar:- itō collukiṉṟām. kēṇmiṉ. (pāṭukiṉṟār)

tirucciṟṟampalam
cucīlaṉ:- vittiyācākarap pulavarē! kavi veku naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu! eṅkē! koñcam arttañ
collum. kēṭpōm.
cūracēnaṉ:- (taṉakkuḷ) aṟiyātu ceytuviṭṭōm. ivviṭattilēyē vittiruntu
kāriyattip pārttirukkavēṇṭum. miñcippōyiṟṟu. iṉimēl eṉṉaceykiṟatu? varuvatu
vantu tāṉē tīrum!
pulavar:- vēppam pūmāliyi yaṇinta caṟkuṇavaḻuti niṉṉuṭiya makimiyi aṟiyātu putti
keṭṭuppōy niṉṉi yetirtta māttirattil avaṉ oru nimiṭattiṟṟāṉē tōṟṟup pōkumpaṭi
tumpip pūmāli yaṇintu pōr purintu avaṉi veṉṟu vāki māli tarittup pāṇarpāṭat
tōkiyar vāḻttac caṇpakap pūvācaṉi poruntiya kūntaliyuṭiya mātarkaḷatu pōkanala
maṉupavittup pirakācikkiṉṟa aḻakāṉa rūpamuḷḷa naṟkuṇamuṭiyavaṉē! pūlōka muḻuvatun
taṉṉuṭiya cakkirātipattiyattiṟkuk kīḻppaṭuttit taṉakkuc camāṉamāṉava revarumillātavaṉē!
maṇaniṟinta paṉampū māliyit taritta cūracēna varmaṉ eṉṟa tivya tirunāmattiyuṭiya makārāja
pūpatiyē! eṉakku aḷavaṟṟa tiraviyattik koṭuttu eṉatu tarittiran tarittirāvaṇṇañ ceytu
nī neṭuṅkālam ciṟappuṭaṉē vāḻvāyāka veṉpatām yām pāṭiya ceyyuḷiṉ poruḷ.
cūracēnaṉ:- cucīlarē! kavi veku naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatē! arttañ colliya piṟpāṭaṉṟō
terikiṉṟatu. pulavarē! nīr kēṭṭuk koṇṭa pirakāram ummuṭiya tarittiram nīṅkumpaṭi
patiṉāyiram varākaṉ umakkuk koṭukkumāṟu uttaravu ceytirukkiṉṟōm. peṟṟuk koḷḷum.
pōm.
pulavar:- mikavu vantaṉam. āyiṉum nām puricivāyil kāvalaṉukku ematu pēṟṟiṟ
kāṟpaṅku tarutumeṉac colliyirukkiṉṟōm. ataṉiyuñ cērttuk koṭukkiya āṇiyiṭiṉ
mikavum nalamuṭittāmal.allākkāl appēti makaṉ emmi yatikamāṉa arantikku
āḷākkuvāṉkāṇ.
cucīlaṉ:- pōy vārum! appaṭiyē ceyyac colvōm.
[pulavar pōkiṉṟār.]
tāṅkaḷeṉṉa ivvāṟu kavalippaṭṭuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟīrkaḷ? appaṭiyē yiruntāluntāṉ eṉṉa?
eppoḻutum yām yutta caṅkattarākavē yirukkiṉṟōmē! namakkeṉṉa?
cūracēnaṉ:- namakkoṉṟu milli. yāyiṉum emmuṭiya maṉam eṉṉavō cāntappaṭavilli. yām
ātiyilēyē cākkiratiyā yiruntirukka vēṇṭum.
[cēvakaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ]
cēvakaṉ:- mantiriyavakaḷik kūṭṭippōkumpaṭi avaka vīṭṭilēruntu āḷ vantirukkiṟāṉ.
cūracēnaṉ:- ēṉ! cucīlarē! umatu vīṭṭilētēṉum nīr ceyyavēṇṭiya kāriyamuṇṭō? nīr
pōkavēṇṭiyatu avaciyan tāṉō?
cucīlaṉ:- ām. pōka vēṇṭum.
cūracēnaṉ:- āṉāṟ pōyvārum.
[cucīlaṉ pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
(taṉakkuḷ) cōḻaṉ vīrēntira ṉeṉpavaṉ atikamāṉa cēnāpala muṭiyavaṉō? intac caṟkuṇavaḻutiyiṉ
pēccik kēṭṭu nammītu paṭiyeṭuttu varuvāṉō? nām ātiyārampamutal ituvari
ceytaṉavellām picakēyām. iṉṉuṅ koñcam yōcaṉi ceyyāmaṟ pōṉōm. ivvaḷavu matikēṭarā
yiruppōmeṉṟu eṇṇavē yilli. ematu maṉam orunāḷum ivvāṟu pataṟiṉatilliyē! ivvāṟu
pataṟuvatip pārttāl ētō kēṭu varumpōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟatē!-
(muṉam)
irukkaṭṭum. pātakamilli. appaṭiyē vīrēntira cōḻaṉ caṟkuṇavaḻutikkākap paṭiyeṭuttu
varuvāṉāyiṉ, nām vaḻutimakaṉ ciṟiyiliruppavaṉit tolittup pōṭuvōm. appuṟam mēl
naṭakkap pōkiṟatip pārttuk koḷvōm. kālamatikamāyiṟṟu. iṉi nām pōka vēṇṭum.
[cūracēnaṉ pōkiṉṟāṉ.]
------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 3 / iraṇṭāṅkaḷam

iṭam:- kaṉṉimāṭam
kālam:- naḷḷiravu
pāttiram:- rūpāvati
rūpāvati:-(taṉakkuḷ) aiyō! iṉṟu eṉ takappaṉār eṉatu tāyuṭaṉ pēcik koṇṭiruntatik
kēṭṭatu mutal eṉ maṉañ cakikkavilliyē!- eṉ takappaṉār ceyalkaḷukku vīrēntira cōḻaṉum
paṭi yeṭuppāṉ. iṉṉoru cōḻaṉum paṭiyeṭuppāṉ.-
(muṉam)
* "muṟpakaṟ ceyyiṟ piṟpakal viḷiyum" (43)
eṉṟavaḷ vākkiyam poyttuppōkumō?-ivaṟṟi yellāṅ koñcaṅkūṭa yōciyāmal eṉ takappaṉār
koṭumiyiṉ mēṟ koṭumiyāyc ceyya niṉippatu maṭamiyiṉum maṭamiyām. antō!
ivarataṟiyāmikkeṉṉa ceyvēṉ? – muṉ ceyta koṭu viṉikkē yūrmuḻutum alar
tūṟṟukiṉṟatē!
-----------
* koṉṟi vēntaṉ
appaṭiyirukka ivar maṟupaṭiyum attaṉmiyāṉa ceyal ceyya yōcittal tāñcāka maruntu
kuṭittalpōlum!- ūrār paḻippati yivar kēṭṭu mirārō?- kēṭṭiruntāl ippaṭi yōcaṉi
ceyvāṉēṉ? - avarkku iv vūrār paḻippatu teriyāmal tāṉirukka vēṇṭum!- aiyō!
itikkuṟittu eṉ takappaṉārukku evvāṟuterivippēṉ?- eṉatu tāyiṉmūlamāyt
terivikkalām eṉṟālum carippaṭātē. avaḷ eṉtakappaṉār colvati yellām maṟutturiyāmaṟ
cari cari eṉṟu kūṟupavaḷāvaḷē!- evariṭañcolli yeṉtakappaṉārukkut terivippēṉ?-
avvāṟu avarukkut terivippatu muṭiyātupōyiṉ eṉṉaceyvēṉ!- ō! cuntarānantaṉē!
eṉṉuyirt talivaṉē!- pāṇṭiyar kulatilakaṉē! pintamiḻ valla paramacātuvē! uṉakku eṉmītu
paripūraṇa nēcamirukkiṟateṉpati yuṇarntu koṇ ṭēṉ! ākiyāl uṉṉi maṇantu cukippatu
ennāḷāvatu kūṭum eṉṟu eṇṇi yituvarikkuṅ koñcam āṟutal aṭintiruntēṉ!- iṉi yāṉ
piḻittirēṉ! piḻittirēṉ!- uṉṉuyirkkuk kēṭu cūḻkiṉṟa eṉ takappaṉārit tuṟappēṉ!
iṉṟu mutal yāṉ avarkku makaḷallēṉ!

eṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ vāymoḻiyit tūymoḻiyāyk koṇṭēṉ. ō! eṉatu pirāṇacuntarānantaṉē! nī
piḻittiruntā laṉṟō yāṉum piḻippēṉ? uṉṉiyaṉṟi, yāṉ piḻittiruttal muyaṟkompu
peṟutal pōlum. ātaliṉ eṉ takappaṉār iṉicceyyappōkum koṭuntoḻiṟku nī yiriyākātu
uṉṉit tappuvittaṟkuriya upāyamoṉṟuceyvēṉ:- aiyō! ikkolip pātakatti uṉakku evvāṟu
terivippēṉ? yāriṭañ colliyaṉuppuvēṉ?--nī yirukkuñ ciṟic cāliyiṉiṉṟum
veḷiyēpōvataṟku ōr irakaciyavaḻi yuṇṭeṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭu avvaḻiyi yaṭittuniṟkuṅ
kataviṉittiṟappataṟkuriya tiṟavukōliyum eṉtakappaṉār paṭukkiyaṟiyiliruntu eṭuttuk
koṇṭēṉ. antō! ittiṟavukōli yuṉakku aṉuppuva teppaṭiyō?--eṉ takappaṉār ceyya
niṉittirukkum pātakac ceyalip paṟṟi oru kaṭitattil eṉ kiyeḻuttiṉṟi yeḻuti
akkaṭitattōṭu ittiṟavukōliyuñ cērttu niṉ ciṟiccālikkuṭ pōṭu vēṉ--aiyō!
kāvalāḷar kaṭitattip pārttuviṭṭā leṉṉa ceyvēṉ?-- avarkaḷukkut
teriyātirukkavēṇṭumē!-- appaṭiyē kāvalāḷar pārātiruntu eṉatarumi nāyakaṉ māttiram
kaṭitaṅkaṇṭutiṟavukōṟ peṟṟu eṉṉikkāmiyāyt tavikkumpaṭi yiṅkēyē viṭṭu viṭṭu
avvaḻiyākap pōyviṭṭā leṉṉa ceyvēṉ?--nāṉ ivvaḷavu varuntiyum eṉ eṇṇam muṭiyātu
pōkumē! -- ākiyāl yāṉum uṭaṉpōkum eṇṇattiyumpaṟṟi oruvāṟu
akkaṭitattiṟkuṟippāy eḻutuvēṉ! - antō! avvāṟu yāṉ kūṭamāykkuṟittatu iṉṉatu
tāṉ eṉṟu avaṉuṇarāṉāyiṉeṉceyvēṉ? uyirtuṟakkavē vēṇṭum! ākiyāl avaṉ eṉṉi
vantukāṇumpaṭi eḻutuvēṉ. iṉi yorukaṇamēṉum tāmatiyēṉ! ippoḻutē kaṭitameḻutuvēṉ!
(rūpāvati eḻuntupōy eḻutu karuvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu varukiṉṟāḷ.) aiyō! nāṉ
ituvarikkum āṭavarevarkkum kaṭitam eḻutilēṉē! ākiyāl avarkku eḻutum oḻuṅkum
iṉṉateṉa vuṇarntilēṉē! eṉṉa ceyvēṉ?—
(kaṭitam varikiṉṟāḷ.)
cī! itu cariyāyilli; vēṟu kaṭitantāṉ eḻutavēṇṭum. (kaṭitattik kiḻitteṟikiṉṟāḷ.)
vēṟu kaṭitam varikiṉṟāḷ, cī! iteṉṉa? itilun tavaṟu viḻuntuviṭṭatē! eṉṉa ceykiṟatu
(mīṭṭuṅkiḻikkiṉṟāḷ; iṉṉoru kaṭitam varikiṉṟāḷ)
itu koñcam cariyāyirukkiṉṟatu. itit tāṉ aṉuppavēṇṭum.--tiṟavukōl eṅkē? kāṇēṉ!
ōkō! eḻutukaruvika ḷeṭukkappōṉaviṭattil vittuviṭṭēṉ pōlum.
(rūpāvati eḻuntupōyt tiṟavukōli yeṭuttukkoṇṭu varukiṉṟāḷ.) ittiṟavukōli
yintak kaṭitattiṉ kaṇ vittut tiṟavukōliruppatu teriyāmaṟ curuṭṭi eṉatu
kātalaṉirukkuñ ciṟiccāliyiṉ cāḷarattiṉ vaḻiyāy eṟintuviṭṭu varuvēṉ.
[rūpāvati pōkiṉṟāḷ]
---------------------------------

------------------------------------------------------------------------

aṅkam - 3 / mūṉṟāṅkaḷam

iṭam:- ciṟiccāli
kālam:- naḷḷiravu
pāttiram:- cuntarānantaṉ
cuntarānantaṉ:(taṉakkuḷ)-iṉṟu eṉṉavō? tūkkameṉpatu ciṟitēṉum varavilli. uṭampō
vitirvitirkkiṉṟatu! maṉamō cañcalappaṭukiṉṟatu! eṉṉuṭiya tāytantiya riruvarum
cōḻanāṭṭiṟ cukkiyamāyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu kēḷvippaṭṭum eṉmaṉañ cantōṣappaṭāma
liruppatēṉō?-aiyō! rūpāvati! nīyēyitaṟkuk kāraṇam!-uṉṉiyē yuyirkkātaliyāy peṟṟu
uḷḷam pūrippatum uṇṭō?-
(muṉam)
ētō eṉṉavō? uyarattiliruntu viḻunta cappam kēṭṭatē! irumpuviḻunta captampōlat
tōṉṟiṟṟē! inta naḷḷiravil ivviṭattil attaṉmiyāṉa captaṅ kēṭkak kāraṇamilliyē!
ellāvaṟṟiṟkum eṉṉaveṉṟu eḻuntupōyp pārppēṉ-
(eḻuntu pōyp pārkkiṉṟāṉ.)
intac cāḷarattiṉ camīpattiṟṟāṉ captam kēṭṭatu. ō! atō eṉṉavō? vaṭṭamāyk kiṭakkiṉṟatē!
eṉṉa? kaṭitaccuruḷō? ām.- ataṉōṭu oru tiṟavukōlu mirukkiṉṟatē īteṉṉi āccariyam!-
iti yeṉṉeṉṟu viḷakkēṟṟip pārppēṉ.-
(viḷakkēṟṟip pārkkiṉṟāṉ)
civa perumāṉē! ituvum niṉ tiruviḷiyāṭṭō? niṉṉaruṭ perumiyi yeṉṉeṉṟurippēṉ!
ōkō! yārō kaṭitameḻuti yirukkiṉṟārkaḷ? ivviṭattilēyē yiruntu kaṭitattip
paṭippēṉ. avviṭampōṉāṟ kāvalāḷar veḷicca muṇarntu viḻittukkoḷvārkaḷ.
(kaṭitam paṭikkiṉṟāṉ)

kaṭitam muḻuvatum paṭittuviṭṭēṉ.-ippoḻutu tāṉ intak kaṭitaccuruḷ viḻuntatu.-intak
kaṭitam paṭittatu mutal maṉaṅ kavalippaṭukiṉṟatē!-uṭal viyarkkiṉṟatē!-eṉṉa ceyvēṉ? iti
yevar eḻutiyirukkak kūṭum? eṉṉuṭiya naṇparkaḷil evarēṉum eḻuti yirukkalāmō?-
illi, appaṭi yāṉāl "uṉ antaraṅka nēcarkaḷum iti yaṟiyap paṭātu" eṉṟu eḻutārkaḷē!
mēlum avarkaḷ iṉṟu pūñcōlikku vantiruntārkaḷē. appōtu inta mātiri
ētāvatiruntāl avarkaḷ eṉṉiṭam kuṟippāyāvatu colluvārkaḷē! iṉṉumavarkaḷ in
naḷḷiravil veḷiyēṟa māṭṭārkaḷē! iḵtu irakaciyamāyu mirukkiṉṟatē! piṉṉi yārtām
eḻutiyirukkak kūṭum?-cila nāḷikkumuṉ cantiramukaṉ cila kaṉavāṉkaḷ taṉ vīṭṭiṟku vantu
ētō pēcikkoṇṭatākac coṉṉāṉē! antak kaṉavāṉkaḷi levarēṉu meḻuti
yiruppārō?-irātu. ēṉeṉṟāl aracaṉatu paṭukki yaṟiyiluḷḷa ittiṟavukōl avarkaḷ
